//! Deterministic role populations validated by the ordinary seed boundary.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#132-generated-initial-worlds-and-password-only-test — generated initialization is explicit.
use super::*;
/// No network, credentials, development doors, durable store or scratch files.
fn proposals(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fixture world not reserved; remaining initialization uncovered".into());}
    let mut people=Vec::new();
    for (table,_) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        let role_columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table).into_iter().filter(|c|c=="role"||super::super::privilege::global_principal(bound,table).is_some()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        for column in role_columns{
            {
                for role in super::super::privilege::role_values(bound,table,&column) {for n in 0..2 {people.push((table.clone(),column.clone(),role.clone(),format!("account:security-{table}-{role}-{n}")));}}
            }
        }
    }
    for seat in histories::viewers(bound).into_iter().filter(|p|p.starts_with("service:")&&p!="service:security") {for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-{}-{n}",seat.replace(':',"-"))));}}
    if people.is_empty(){for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-owner-{n}")));}}
    if people.len()>64{people.truncate(64);out.unsupported.insert("generated roster capped at 64 principals; additional roles remain uncovered".into());}
    let mut grant_tables=BTreeSet::new();
    fn grants(rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|v|matches!(v,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{out.insert(table.clone());},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{grants(r,out)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>grants(r,out),_=>{}
    }}
    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){grants(&t.rules.read,&mut grant_tables);}
    let counts=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).map(|(table,t)|{
        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).count();(table.clone(),if own>0{own}else if grant_tables.contains(table)||t.rules.read.any_term(&|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if t.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)))){people.len().max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3))}else{t.columns.values().filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(2).max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3)).clamp(2,8)})
    }).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    let mut rows=BTreeMap::new();
    for (table,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        if !counts.contains_key(table){continue;}
        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count()>3 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: full Cartesian combinations above three principal columns remain uncovered"));}
        let mut population=Vec::new();
        for n in 0..counts[table]{
            let person=if own.is_empty(){&people[n%people.len()]}else{own[n]};
            let mut fields=BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),Value::Id(format!("security-{table}-{n}")))]);
            let mut principal=0;
            for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
                if column=="id"||t.engine_field(column)||t.recorded.contains_key(column)||t.maintained.contains_key(column){continue;}
                let mut value=if kind.is_optional()&&n%2==0&&!(person.0==*table&&person.1==*column){Value::Null}else{match sample(kind.required(),4+n){Some(value)=>value,None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}.{column}: unsupported typed sample"));continue;}}};
                if value==Value::Null&&kind.is_optional(){fields.insert(column.clone(),value);continue;}
                match kind.required(){
                    C::Principal=>{
                        let linked=!own.is_empty()||grant_tables.contains(table)||t.constraints.iter().any(|constraint|matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{key,..} if key.iter().any(|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column))));
                        value=Value::Id(if linked{if principal==0{person.3.clone()}else{people[(n+principal)%people.len()].3.clone()}}else{if (n>>principal.min(5))&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()});principal+=1;
                    },
                    C::Ref{table:target}=>{value=Value::Id(format!("security-{target}-{}",n%counts.get(target).copied().unwrap_or(1)));},
                    C::Enum{..}|C::Text{..} if person.0==*table&&person.1==*column=>value=Value::String(person.2.clone()),
                    C::Text{max}=>{let minimum=t.constraints.iter().filter_map(|c|if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=c{(field==column).then_some(*min as usize)}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(1);let suffix=format!("sample{n}");value=Value::String(format!("{suffix}{}","x".repeat(minimum.saturating_sub(suffix.len()))).chars().take(*max as usize).collect());},
                    C::Time=>value=Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_deref()==Some(column){86_400_000}else{-1000}),
                    _=>{},
                }
                fields.insert(column.clone(),value);
            }
            population.push(Value::Row(fields.into()));
        }
        rows.insert(table.clone(),population);
    }
    // Batch validation preserves final-exists, parent ordering and maintains.
    if let Ok(engine)=engine_with(bound,&rows,Vec::new()){return Ok(engine);}
    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fallback world not reserved; initialization uncovered".into());}
    let mut engine=engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new())?;
    let mut pending=rows.into_iter().flat_map(|(table,rows)|rows.into_iter().map(move|row|(table.clone(),row))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut reasons=BTreeMap::new();
    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
        for (table,mut row) in pending {
            if !out.reserve(1){validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
            let attempt=(||{
                for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&table].columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column){let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,value)?;if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut row{fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}}}
                engine.seed_at([(table.clone(),row.clone())],NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;Ok::<_,String>(())
            })();
            if let Err(why)=attempt{reasons.insert(table.clone(),why);retry.push((table,row));}
        }
        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
    }
    for (table,why) in reasons{if pending.iter().any(|(name,_)|name==&table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: some related rows declined by ordinary validation: {why}"));}}
    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — creator guards/copies are part of generated initialization.
pub fn fixture(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
    let proposed=proposals(bound,out)?;
    let rows=readback(&proposed)?;
    let actors=histories::actors(bound,&rows);
    let rosters=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(name,t)|t.columns.contains_key("role")||super::super::privilege::global_principal(bound,name).is_some()&&!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()).map(|(name,_)|name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let creators=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&p.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)&&ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert"))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let created=creators.iter().flat_map(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program)).filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut engine=engine_on_store(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new(),proposed.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
    let mut pending=rows.iter().filter(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(*t)||!created.contains(*t)).flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    // Bootstrap proposals require an actor admitted by the insert rule in the
    // previous world. Never let a proposed role authorize its own insertion.
    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
        for (table,row) in pending{
            if !out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated initialization work exhausted; remaining populations uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
            let gate=&bound.schema.tables[&table].rules.insert;
            if !actors.iter().any(|actor|matches!(engine.fixture_policy(gate,actor,None,&row,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Readable))) {retry.push((table,row));continue;}
            let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            let seeded=clean_row(bound,&table,&row);
            if candidate.seed_at([(table.clone(),seeded)],NOW).is_ok(){engine=candidate;}else{retry.push((table,row));}
        }
        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
    }
    for (table,_) in pending{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: no admitted insert/bootstrap; population uncovered"));}
    // Authored insertion bodies preserve requires, immutable copies, defaults,
    // role checks on principal arguments and multi-table creation invariants.
    let mut attempts=0usize;
    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len().min(12){
        let before=readback(&engine)?;
        for bp in &creators{
            let p=&bp.program;let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
            if writes.iter().all(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(t)){continue;}
            let current=readback(&engine)?;
            let targets=writes.iter().filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            if targets.iter().all(|t|current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){continue;}
            let cases=histories::fixture_cases(p,&current,&actors,out);
            'cases:for args in cases.into_iter().take(16){for actor in &actors{
                if attempts>=2048||!out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated authored initialization capped at 2048 calls; remaining creators uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
                attempts+=1;
                let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut candidate,&p.args,&args,actor){Ok(a)=>a,Err(_)=>continue};
                let result=candidate.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(actor,args).at(NOW));
                if matches!(result,Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})) {
                    let after=readback(&candidate)?;
                    if targets.iter().any(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)) {engine=candidate;}
                    let after=readback(&engine)?;
                    if targets.iter().all(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){break 'cases;}
                }
            }}
        }
        if readback(&engine)?==before{break;}
    }
    for table in created.difference(&rosters){if engine.store().rows(table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: authored creator did not populate this table"));}}
    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;Ok(engine)
}
fn clean_row(bound:&Bound,table:&str,row:&Value)->Value{
    let t=&bound.schema.tables[table];
    match row{Value::Row(fields)=>Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect()),_=>row.clone()}
}

+// Counterfactual field mutation must not break an authored creator's copies
+// or recipient guards. Direct typed payload observations run independently.
+fn coupled(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->bool{
+    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
+    let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
+    if table.rules.insert.needs_field_value(column,&table.columns[column])||table.update_rule_judged().needs_field_value(column,&table.columns[column]){return true;}
+    fn definitions(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>){
+        if let Some(object)=value.as_object(){
+            for tag in ["Const","Let","Assign"]{if let Some(binding)=object.get(tag)&&let (Some(name),Some(value))=(binding.get("name").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binding.get("value")){
+                if tag=="Assign"||out.contains_key(name){out.insert(name.into(),serde_json::Value::Null);}else{out.insert(name.into(),value.clone());}
+            }}
+            for child in object.values(){definitions(child,out);}
+        }else if let Some(xs)=value.as_array(){for child in xs{definitions(child,out);}}
+    }
+    fn fields<'a>(expression:&'a serde_json::Value,bindings:&'a BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,depth:usize)->Option<&'a serde_json::Map<String,serde_json::Value>>{
+        if depth>32{return None;}
+        if let Some(map)=expression.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return Some(map);}
+        if let Some(projection)=expression.get("Builtin")&&projection.get("builtin").is_some_and(|b|b.get("ProjectFields").is_some()){
+            return fields(projection.get("args")?.as_array()?.first()?,bindings,depth+1);
+        }
+        if let Some(name)=expression.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str){return fields(bindings.get(name)?,bindings,depth+1);}
+        None
+    }
+    fn dependency(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,depth:usize)->bool{
+        if depth>32{return true;}
+        if let Some(field)=value.get("Field")&&let (Some(base),Some(name))=(field.get("base"),field.get("name").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str))&&let Some(map)=fields(base,bindings,depth+1){
+            return map.get(name).is_none_or(|expression|dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1));
+        }
+        if let Some(name)=value.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str){
+            if name==arg{return true;}
+            if let Some(bound)=bindings.get(name){return bound.is_null()||dependency(bound,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1);}
+            return !args.contains_key(name);
+        }
+        // A guard re-reading the written table may inspect the supplied field
+        // through a row alias even when the argument itself occurs only once.
+        for tag in ["Get","Exists"]{if value.get(tag).and_then(|v|v.get("table")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(table){return true;}}
+        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|dependency(v,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|dependency(v,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1)),_=>false}
+    }
+    fn guarded(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>)->bool{
+        for tag in ["Require","Check","If"]{if let Some(condition)=value.get(tag).and_then(|v|v.get("condition"))&&dependency(condition,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}}
+        for tag in ["Binary","Unary","Builtin","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"]{if let Some(expression)=value.get(tag){
+            if tag=="Builtin"&&expression.get("builtin").is_some_and(|b|b.get("ProjectFields").is_some()){continue;}
+            if dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}
+        }}
+        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|guarded(v,arg,table,args,bindings)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|guarded(v,arg,table,args,bindings)),_=>false}
+    }
+    fn related_write(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,column:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>)->bool{
+        for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
+            let Some(write)=value.get(tag)else{continue;};
+            let name=write.get("table").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
+            if let Some(row)=write.get("row").or_else(||write.get("patch")){
+                if let Some(map)=fields(row,bindings,0){
+                    if map.iter().any(|(field,expression)|(name!=Some(table)||field!=column)&&dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,0)){return true;}
+                }else if dependency(row,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}
+            }
+            if write.get("key").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|target|target.get("key"))).is_some_and(|key|dependency(key,arg,table,args,bindings,0))||write.get("condition").is_some_and(|condition|dependency(condition,arg,table,args,bindings,0)){return true;}
+        }
+        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|related_write(v,arg,table,column,args,bindings)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|related_write(v,arg,table,column,args,bindings)),_=>false}
+    }
+    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,table:&str,column:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,program:&str,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,guards:&BTreeSet<String>,defaulted:bool,found:&mut bool,coupled:&mut bool){
+        if let Some(object)=value.as_object(){
+            for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
+                let Some(write)=object.get(tag)else{continue;};
+                let name=write.get("table").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
+                if name!=Some(table){continue;}
+                let map=write.get("row").or_else(||write.get("patch")).and_then(|e|fields(e,bindings,0));
+                let Some(map)=map else{*found=true;*coupled=true;continue;};
+                if let Some(expression)=map.get(column){
+                    *found=true;
+                    if let Some(name)=expression.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)&&args.contains_key(name){
+                        let occurrence=serde_json::to_string(&Expr::Variable(name.into())).unwrap();
+                        if program.matches(&occurrence).count()!=1||guards.contains(name){*coupled=true;}
+                    }else{*coupled=true;}
+                }else if tag=="Upsert"||defaulted&&matches!(tag,"Insert"|"InsertIfAbsent"){*found=true;*coupled=true;}
+            }
+            for child in object.values(){writes(child,table,column,args,program,bindings,guards,defaulted,found,coupled);}
+        }else if let Some(xs)=value.as_array(){for child in xs{writes(child,table,column,args,program,bindings,guards,defaulted,found,coupled);}}
+    }
+    let(mut found,mut unsafe_writer)=(false,false);
+    for p in &bound.programs{let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();let text=serde_json::to_string(&p.program).unwrap();let mut bindings=BTreeMap::new();definitions(&json,&mut bindings);let guards=p.program.args.keys().filter(|arg|guarded(&json,arg,&inv.table,&p.program.args,&bindings)||related_write(&json,arg,&inv.table,column,&p.program.args,&bindings)).cloned().collect();writes(&json,&inv.table,column,&p.program.args,&text,&bindings,&guards,table.defaults.contains_key(column),&mut found,&mut unsafe_writer);}
+    found&&unsafe_writer
+}

//! Positive campaign evidence only; never a cached security proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — exact source/fixture/executable binding.
use super::*;
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
#[derive(serde::Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub(super) struct Record{pub fingerprint:String,pub complete:bool,pub attempted:usize,pub sequences:usize,pub coverage_gaps:usize,pub violations:Vec<security::challenges::Counterexample>}
fn selected_fixture(project:&Project,options:&Options)->std::path::PathBuf{
    let flag=if options.flags.contains_key("source"){"source"}else{"schema"};
    options.flags.get("fixture").map(std::path::PathBuf::from).unwrap_or_else(||{
        let saved=options.flags.get(flag).map(std::path::Path::new).map(|p|if p.is_dir(){p.join("fixtures")}else if p.file_name().is_some_and(|n|n=="schema.q"){p.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures")}else{p.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures",p.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy()))});
        saved.filter(|p|p.is_dir()).unwrap_or_else(||project.root.join("fixtures"))
    })
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn capture(sources:&[(String,String)],fixture:Option<&std::path::Path>)->Result<String,String>{
    let mut hash=Sha256::new();hash.update(b"SEC4-test-record-v2/full-default/NOW=1800000000000");
    for (name,text) in sources{hash.update((name.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(name);hash.update((text.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(text);}
    let binary=std::env::current_exe().and_then(std::fs::read).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;hash.update(Sha256::digest(binary));
    if let Some(path)=fixture.filter(|p|p.is_dir()){
        let mut files=std::fs::read_dir(path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;files.sort_by_key(|e|e.file_name());
        for f in files{
            // Follow the same regular-file symlinks as the fixture loader.
            if !std::fs::metadata(f.path()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_file(){continue;}
            let name=f.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();let bytes=std::fs::read(f.path()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            hash.update((name.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(name);hash.update((bytes.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(bytes);
        }
    }Ok(format!("{:x}",hash.finalize()))
}
fn fingerprint(project:&Project,options:&Options)->Result<String,String>{
    let flag=if options.flags.contains_key("source"){"source"}else{"schema"};
    capture(&super::super::proof_source::sources(project,options,flag)?,Some(&selected_fixture(project,options)))
}
fn path(project:&Project)->std::path::PathBuf{project.root.join("snapback/generated/security-test.json")}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn remember(project:&Project,options:&Options,campaign:&security::challenges::Campaign,complete:bool,consumed:&str)->Result<(),String>{
    if fingerprint(project,options)?!=consumed{return Err("source or fixtures changed during test; evidence was not saved; rerun snapback4 test".into());}
    let mut record=Record{fingerprint:consumed.into(),complete,attempted:campaign.attempted,sequences:campaign.sequences,coverage_gaps:campaign.unsupported.len(),violations:campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable()).cloned().collect()};
    let prior=matching(project,options).map(|r|r.violations).unwrap_or_default();
    let mut seen=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    record.violations=prior.into_iter().chain(record.violations).filter(|v|v.reachability.is_reachable()&&seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&v.invariant_site,&v.viewer,&v.operation,&v.effect,&v.reachability)).unwrap())).collect();
    let path=path(project);std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let temp=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
    let result=std::fs::write(&temp,serde_json::to_vec(&record).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?).and_then(|_|std::fs::rename(&temp,&path));
    if result.is_err(){let _=std::fs::remove_file(temp);}result.map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
pub(super) fn matching(project:&Project,options:&Options)->Option<Record>{
    let path=path(project);
    let record:Record=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(path).ok()?).ok()?;
    (record.fingerprint==fingerprint(project,options).ok()?).then_some(record)
}

 #[test]
 fn a_witness_free_later_run_keeps_failures_and_does_not_promote_synthetic_evidence(){
  let path=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-security-record-{}",std::process::id()));
  struct Scratch(std::path::PathBuf);impl Drop for Scratch{fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
  let _scratch=Scratch(path.clone());std::fs::create_dir_all(path.join("snapback")).unwrap();
  let source="table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant docs.read only .owner = viewer\n";
  std::fs::write(path.join("snapback/schema.q"),source).unwrap();let project=Project{root:path};let options=Options{positionals:Vec::new(),flags:Default::default(),json:false};
  let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
  let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::memory(bound.schema.clone(),bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
  engine.seed_at([("docs".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Row([("id".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Id("doc".into())),("owner".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Id("dev:owner".into()))].into_iter().collect()))],security::challenges::NOW).unwrap();
  let mut c=security::challenges::Campaign::default();security::challenges::fixture_observers(&bound,&engine,&mut c);assert!(!c.violations.is_empty());
  let mut synthetic=c.violations[0].clone();synthetic.reachability=security::challenges::Reachability::SyntheticOperation;synthetic.operation="synthetic-must-not-persist".into();c.violations.push(synthetic);
  let consumed=fingerprint(&project,&options).unwrap();
  remember(&project,&options,&c,true,&consumed).unwrap();let prior=matching(&project,&options).unwrap();assert!(prior.violations.iter().all(|v|v.reachability.is_reachable()));
  remember(&project,&options,&Default::default(),false,&consumed).unwrap();let next=matching(&project,&options).unwrap();assert!(!next.complete);assert_eq!(next.violations.len(),prior.violations.len());
 }
}

//! Saved-source fixture selection, business cases and the query sweep.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#9-wave-13-saved-version-proofs-day3-2026-10-16 — contemporary fixtures belong to the selected saved source.
use super::*;

const CASE_SUMMARY: &str = "SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES";

fn case_count(path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
    Ok(text.lines().filter(|line| {
        let line = line.trim();
        !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with("//")
    }).count())
}

/// `test`: run every argument-free query as each persona against the store
/// (optionally loaded from a fixture) and print the meters beside the
/// ceilings, so a builder reads numbers instead of being refused on a bound.
pub fn test(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    let project = match proof_source::project(options, "source") { Ok(project) => project, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let sources = match proof_source::sources(&project, options, "source") { Ok(sources) => sources, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let bound = compiled_or_exit(proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "source"), options.json);
    let mut defaults = options.clone();
    let mut skipped_business_cases = 0;
    if !defaults.flags.contains_key("fixture") {
        let saved = options.flags.get("source").map(std::path::Path::new).map(|path| {
            if path.is_dir() { path.join("fixtures") }
            else if path.file_name().is_some_and(|name| name == "schema.q") { path.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures") }
            else { path.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures", path.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())) }
        });
        if let Some(fixture) = saved.filter(|path| path.is_dir()) {
            eprintln!("test: using {} (saved version's contemporary fixtures; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display());
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir() {
            let fixture = project.root.join("fixtures");
            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
                defaults.flags.insert("saved-fixture-fallback".into(), String::new());
                eprintln!("test: no contemporary fixtures for saved source; validating only declared-table files in {}; pass --fixture <dir> for a complete contemporary case suite", fixture.display());
                let cases = fixture.join(test_cases::FILE);
                if cases.is_file() {
                    skipped_business_cases = match case_count(&cases) { Ok(count) => count, Err(error) => { eprintln!("test: {error}"); return 1; } };
                    eprintln!("test: skipped {} — working cases may target another release; pass --fixture <dir> to run explicitly", cases.display());
                }
            } else { eprintln!("test: using {} (default fixture directory; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display()); }
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            eprintln!("test: no saved or working fixtures; query sweep only; pass --fixture <dir> for business cases");
        }
    }
    let evidence_options=defaults.clone();
    let fixture_inputs=match defaults.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_|!options.flags.contains_key("residency")){
        Some(path)=>match test_fixture::snapshot(std::path::Path::new(path)){Ok(snapshot)=>Some(snapshot),Err(why)=>{eprintln!("test fixture snapshot: {why}");return 1;}},None=>None,
    };
    if let Some(snapshot)=&fixture_inputs {defaults.flags.insert("fixture-input-label".into(),defaults.flags["fixture"].clone());defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(),snapshot.0.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    let consumed=if options.flags.contains_key("residency"){String::new()}else{match security::capture(&sources,fixture_inputs.as_ref().map(|s|s.0.as_path())){Ok(key)=>key,Err(why)=>{eprintln!("test input fingerprint: {why}");return 1;}}};
    let options = &defaults;
    // test applies dev's cast: a persona dev would refuse is not run as an ordinary viewer.
    // Residency measures any principal in the data (scale fixtures name thousands), so it notes instead.
    if let Some(why) = options.flags.get("as").and_then(|names| names.split(',').find_map(|name| test_cases::undeclared_persona(name, &bound.personas))) {
        if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
            eprintln!("test --as: {why}");
            return 1;
        }
        eprintln!("test --residency --as: measuring an undeclared principal; dev would refuse it as a persona");
    }
    let _owner = if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") || options.flags.contains_key("source") || !options.flags.contains_key("residency") { None } else {
        match crate::store_owner::command(&project.store_path()) {
            Ok(owner) => Some(owner),
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    };
    let fixture_now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let opened = match &_owner {
        Some(owner) => owner.open_engine(&bound, std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), fixture_now).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&!options.flags.contains_key("residency") => {
            snapback4_core::Store::memory().and_then(|store|snapback4_core::Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()),fixture_now)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
        },
        None => test_fixture::engine_at(&project, &bound, options, fixture_now),
    };
    let mut engine = match opened {
        Ok(engine) => engine,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    };
    let personas = residency::personas(&bound, options);
    let sweep_personas = if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") { Default::default() } else {
        match test_sweep::load(options.flags.get("fixture"), &bound) {
            Ok(matrix) => matrix,
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    };
    if let Some(persona) = options.flags.get("as") && !sweep_personas.is_empty() {
        println!("test --as {persona} overrides test.sweep.json because --as requests one deliberate global probe; for: {}", sweep_personas.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
        let verbose = options.flags.contains_key("verbose");
        if let Err(error) = residency::measure(&mut engine, &bound, &personas, verbose) { eprintln!("residency measurement failed: {error}"); return 1; }
        if !verbose {
            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
                println!("{CASE_SUMMARY} {{\"run\":0,\"skipped\":{skipped_business_cases}}}");
            }
            if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture-only") {
                eprintln!("test --residency executed no business cases; use --fixture-only for acquisition measurement, or omit --residency to run the cases");
                return 1;
            }
            return 0;
        }
    }
    let mut security_campaign = snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Campaign::default();
    let populated_fixture=bound.schema.tables.keys().any(|table|engine.store().rows(table).is_ok_and(|rows|!rows.is_empty()));
    if options.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&populated_fixture{
        match test_fixture::engine_at(&project,&bound,options,snapback4_lang::security::challenges::NOW){
            Ok(security_fixture)=>snapback4_lang::security::challenges::operations(&bound,&security_fixture,&mut security_campaign),
            Err(why)=>{security_campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("trusted fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
        }
    }else{
        snapback4_lang::security::challenges::generated_campaign(&bound,&mut security_campaign,true);
    }
    let security_campaign=snapback4_lang::security::challenges::run_selected_after(&bound,None,security_campaign);
    let mut security_campaign=security_campaign;
    let security_http_ok=security::unauthenticated(&bound,&sources);
    if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {if let Err(why)=security::remember(&project,&evidence_options,&security_campaign,security_http_ok,&consumed){eprintln!("security: campaign record unavailable: {why}; check cannot reuse these refutations");}}
    if security_http_ok{security_campaign.unsupported.retain(|s|!s.starts_with("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require"));}
    println!("security histories: {} ordered short operation sequences replayed",security_campaign.sequences);
    for why in &security_campaign.boundary_failures {println!("security boundary failure: {why}");}
    println!("security operation matrix: {} operations exercised; unknown authorization intent is not inferred from success",security_campaign.operations.len());
    let reachable=security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable()).count();
    println!("security tests: {} attempted, {} refused, {reachable} reachable violations; {} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing); finite campaign, not proof",security_campaign.attempted,security_campaign.refused,security_campaign.violations.len()-reachable);
    for c in security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|options.flags.contains_key("all")||c.reachability.is_reachable()) {println!("security violation: {}",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap());}
    for reason in &security_campaign.unsupported {println!("security not covered: {reason}");}
    let (mut passed, mut failed_cases) = (0, 0);
    if let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_| !options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback")) {
        match test_cases::run_verbose(&mut engine, &bound, fixture, &sources, fixture_now, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")) {
            Ok((p, f)) => { passed = p; failed_cases = f; }
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    }
    let ceilings = engine.ceilings();
    let mut failures = 0;
    println!("test: {:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}", "operation (persona)", "examined", "entries", "probes", "steps", "heap B", "output B");
    println!("      {:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}", "ceilings", ceilings.examined, ceilings.index_entries, ceilings.rule_probes, ceilings.steps, ceilings.heap_bytes, ceilings.output_bytes);
    println!("legend: examined=rows visited; entries=index visits; probes=rule lookups; steps=interpreter work; heap B=charged heap; output B=encoded answer.");
    println!("verdicts: complete=whole answer; capped=partial; refused=expected-case or program-require refusal; denied=unexpected refusal; not run=no coverage; case OK=expectation passed.");
    let mut skipped = Vec::new();
    let mut held_not_run = 0;
    let mut near = Vec::new();
    let (mut complete, mut capped, mut refused_derived, mut expected) = (0usize, 0usize, 0usize, 0usize);
    let explicit = options.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_| !options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback")).map(|fixture| test_cases::explicit(fixture, &bound, fixture_now)).unwrap_or_default();
    for program in &bound.programs {
        if program.program.kind != ProgramKind::Query {
            continue;
        }
//! CLI security reporting; compile/test evidence share one analyzer.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#5-exposure-report — full audit is opt-in, intent notes are on ordinary check.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::security::{self,Report};
mod http;
mod record;
pub(super) use record::{remember,capture};
pub(super) fn unauthenticated(bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    match http::run(bound,sources){Ok(_)=>true,Err(why)=>{eprintln!("security HTTP failure: {why}");false}}
}
pub(super) fn report(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,options:&Options)->Report{
    let mut report=security::analyze(bound);
    if let Ok(sources)=project.sources(){security::add_source_notes(&mut report,&sources,bound);}
    let cached=record::matching(project,options);
    let known_failure=cached.as_ref().is_some_and(|r|!r.violations.is_empty())&&!options.flags.contains_key("security-full");
    let mut campaign=if options.flags.contains_key("security-full"){security::challenges::Campaign::default()}else{security::challenges::Campaign::bounded(1500)};
    if known_failure {campaign.work_remaining=Some(0);campaign.unsupported.insert("matching full-test refutations retained; bounded resampling skipped; repair and rerun snapback4 test".into());}
    if !bound.invariants.is_empty()&&!known_failure{
        let mut selection=options.clone();
        if !selection.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir(){selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),project.root.join("fixtures").to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
        if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
            match super::test_fixture::engine_at(project,bound,&selection,1_800_000_000_000){
                Ok(fixture)=>if bound.schema.tables.keys().any(|t|fixture.store().rows(t).is_ok_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())){security::challenges::fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,&mut campaign)}else{security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false)},
                Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));}
            }
        }else{security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false);}
    }
    let sites=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&!campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.invariant_site==v.invariant.site.to_string()&&c.reachability.is_reachable())).map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect();
    let campaign=security::challenges::run_selected_after(bound,Some(&sites),campaign);
    security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&campaign);
    if let Some(record)=cached {security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&security::challenges::Campaign{violations:record.violations,..Default::default()});}
    report
}
pub fn audit(options:&Options)->i32{
    let project=project_or_exit();
    let bound=compiled_or_exit(match project.compile(){Ok(b)=>b,Err(e)=>{eprintln!("audit: {e}");return 2;}},options.json);
    let mut full=options.clone();full.flags.insert("security-full".into(),String::new());
    let report=report(&project,&bound,&full);
    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::to_string(&report).unwrap());}
    else {print!("{}",report.text_with_all(options.flags.contains_key("all")));}
    if report.refuted(){1}else{0}
}
pub(super) fn exposure(report:&Report){
    let broad=report.exposures.iter().filter(|e|e.effects.get("read").is_some_and(|r|r=="allow"||r.starts_with("public"))).count();
    eprintln!("security exposure: {} tables, {} conditional audiences, {broad} broadly readable tables; run snapback4 audit for exact role/column predicates",report.exposures.len(),report.roles.len());
}
pub(super) fn summary(report:&Report,verbose:bool){
    exposure(report);
    let proved=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Proved).count();
    let refuted=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
    let supplemental=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&!v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
    eprintln!("security: {proved} proved, {refuted} refuted, {} unproved; {supplemental} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing)",report.invariants.len()-proved-refuted);
    for v in report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted){
        if let Some(c)=&v.counterexample&&c.reachability.is_reachable(){eprintln!("  {} FAIL {} via {} ({:?}; initial fixture assumptions apply)",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.target_label(),c.operation,c.reachability);}
    }
    if verbose {eprint!("{}",report.text_with_all(true));}
    if !report.invariants.is_empty()||!report.campaign_coverage.is_empty(){eprintln!("  run snapback4 test for full campaigns; {} coverage gaps (--verbose/JSON for details)",report.campaign_coverage.len());}
    if !report.exposures.is_empty(){eprintln!("  run snapback4 audit for column audiences, grant writers and {} uncovered exposures",report.uncovered.len());}
}

/// A rule-changing preflight demands a current campaign, without pretending
/// finite tests or undeclared intent certify a deployment.
pub(super) fn release_loop(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,prior:Option<&Bound>,options:&Options){
    let changed=prior.is_some_and(|old|bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(name,t)|old.schema.tables.get(name).is_none_or(|o|serde_json::to_value(&o.rules).ok()!=serde_json::to_value(&t.rules).ok()))||old.schema.tables.keys().any(|t|!bound.schema.tables.contains_key(t)));
    if !changed{return;}
    let report=security::analyze(bound);exposure(&report);
    let selection=options.flags.get("schema").map(|s|format!(" --source {s:?}")).unwrap_or_default();
    let fixture=options.flags.get("fixture").map(|s|format!(" --fixture {s:?}")).unwrap_or_default();
    match record::matching(project,options){
        Some(record) if record.complete=>eprintln!("security rules changed: matching test campaign recorded; {} reachable refutations; {} coverage gaps; {} independent invariants (finite, not proof)",record.violations.len(),record.coverage_gaps,bound.invariants.len()),
        _=>eprintln!("security rules changed: run `snapback4 test{selection}{fixture}` before deployment; no matching completed campaign for this source, fixtures and CLI; {} independent invariants",bound.invariants.len()),
    }
}

//! Concrete, isolated challenges; finite campaigns never establish proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#4-counterexamples-and-generated-tests — only replayed admission is a refutation.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Engine,Store,Context,Value,RuntimeError};
use snapback4_core::schema::{ColumnType as C,Constraint};
use snapback4_core::engine::rules::{self,Probe};
use snapback4_core::ir::{self,Expr,Stmt,Program,ProgramKind,Read,KeyProvenance};
pub const NOW:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
const VIEWER:&str="dev:security-a";
const OTHER:&str="dev:security-b";
// Same verified 1x1 PNG header used by the existing packaged asset witnesses.
const PNG:[u8;24]=[137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10,0,0,0,13,73,72,68,82,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1];

#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize,PartialEq,Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="kebab-case")]
pub enum Reachability { AuthoredFixtureTrace, GeneratedFixtureTrace, TrustedSeedOnly, SyntheticOperation }
impl Reachability {
    pub fn is_reachable(&self)->bool {matches!(self,Self::AuthoredFixtureTrace|Self::GeneratedFixtureTrace)}
}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Counterexample {pub reachability:Reachability,pub invariant_site:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub viewer:String,pub operation:String,pub effect:String,pub row:Value,pub old:Option<Value>,pub supporting_rows:BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,pub rule_path:String,pub scope:String, pub observations:Option<paired::Observations>,pub history:Vec<histories::Step>}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Default,Serialize)]
pub struct Campaign {#[serde(skip)] pub history_limit:usize,#[serde(skip)] pub generated_fixture:bool,#[serde(skip)] pub work_remaining:Option<usize>,#[serde(skip)] pub deadline:Option<std::time::Instant>,pub paired_observer_calls:usize,pub history_observer_calls:usize,pub column_observer_calls:usize,pub operations:BTreeMap<String,usize>,pub sequences:usize,pub attempted:usize,pub admitted:usize,pub refused:usize,pub violations:Vec<Counterexample>,pub boundary_failures:Vec<String>,pub unsupported:BTreeSet<String>}
impl Campaign {
    pub fn bounded(milliseconds:u64)->Self {Self{work_remaining:Some(512),deadline:Some(std::time::Instant::now()+std::time::Duration::from_millis(milliseconds)),..Self::default()}}
    pub(super) fn reserve(&mut self,cost:usize)->bool {
        if self.exhausted(){return false;}
        if let Some(remaining)=&mut self.work_remaining {
            if *remaining<cost{*remaining=0;self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration work budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());return false;}
            *remaining-=cost;
        }true
    }
    pub(super) fn exhausted(&mut self)->bool {
        if self.work_remaining==Some(0){self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration work budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());return true;}
        if self.deadline.is_some_and(|deadline|std::time::Instant::now()>=deadline){self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration time budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());true}else{false}
    }
}
fn id(table:&str)->Value {Value::Id(format!("security-{table}"))}
fn sample(kind:&C,variant:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match kind{
    C::Optional(inner)=>if variant&4!=0{sample(inner,variant).unwrap_or(Value::Null)}else{Value::Null},C::Id|C::Principal=>Value::Id(if variant&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()),
    C::Ref{table}=>id(table),C::Text{max}=>Value::String("x".repeat((*max).min(20) as usize)),
    C::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(variant%variants.len())?.clone()),
    C::Int|C::Decimal{..}|C::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),C::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),
    C::Bool=>Value::Bool((variant>>2)&1==0),C::Time=>Value::Int(NOW-1000),C::Json{..}=>Value::Null,C::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),
    C::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),C::ListRange{of,min,..}=>Value::Array((0..*min).map(|_|sample(of,variant)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    C::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),C::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),C::Video{..}=>return None,
})}
fn needed(schema:&Schema,name:&str,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){
    if !out.insert(name.into()){return;}let Some(t)=schema.table(name)else{return};
    for m in schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.target==name){needed(schema,&m.source,out);}
    for k in t.columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=k.required(){needed(schema,table,out);}}
    for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){needed(schema,&dep,out);}}
}
fn snapshot(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,false)
}
fn snapshot_mode(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize,timed:bool)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    let mut names=BTreeSet::new();needed(&bound.schema,&inv.table,&mut names);
    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy{for dep in kernel::dependencies(policy){needed(&bound.schema,&dep,&mut names);}}
    if let Some(mut syntax)=inv.syntax.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut syntax,&mut |e| {
        if let ast::Expr::Read(r)|ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=e {needed(&bound.schema,&r.table,&mut names);}
    });}
    if let Some(op)=&inv.operation && let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op){for kind in p.program.args.values(){if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind{needed(&bound.schema,table,&mut names);}}}
    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
    let mut enum_case=variant;
    for name in names {
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&name];
        if bound.schema.maintain_for(&name).is_some(){continue;}
        let mut fields=BTreeMap::new();fields.insert("id".into(),id(&name));
        let mut principal_index=0usize;
        for (n,k) in &t.columns{
            if n=="id"||t.engine_field(n)||t.maintained.contains_key(n){continue;}
            let sample_variant=if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){let n=enum_case % variants.len();enum_case/=variants.len();n}else if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal){let n=((variant>>((if name==inv.table{0}else{3})+principal_index.min(5)))&1)|(variant&4);principal_index+=1;n}else{variant};
            let value=sample(k,sample_variant)
                .ok_or_else(||format!("{name}.{n}: asset ledger/required sample is not available"))?;
            fields.insert(n.clone(),value);
        }
        if timed || t.read_lifetime_sensitive() {for (n,k) in &t.columns {if matches!(k.required(),C::Time)&&(!k.is_optional()||variant&4!=0) {
            fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_ref()==Some(n){2000}else if timed{1000}else{-1000}));
        }}}
        for constraint in &t.constraints{if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=constraint
            && matches!(fields.get(field),Some(Value::String(_))) {fields.insert(field.clone(),Value::String("x".repeat((*min).max(1) as usize)));}}
        // Named primary keys used by role probes must select the challenged
        // viewer; changing the enum represents a current-role change while
        // derived assignment/membership rows remain present.
        if name!=inv.table {for (i,(n,k)) in t.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,C::Principal)).enumerate(){fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Id(if variant & (1 << (i+3)) == 0 {VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into())); let _=k;}}
        out.insert(name,vec![Value::Row(fields.into())]);
    }
    Ok(out)
}
struct Snapshot<'a>{schema:&'a Schema,rows:&'a BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>}
impl Probe for Snapshot<'_>{
    fn exists(&mut self,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[Value],equalities:&[(String,Value)])->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
        let t=&self.schema.tables[table];let Some(idx)=t.index(index)else{return Ok(false)};
        if idx.components.len()!=key.len(){return Ok(false);}
        if idx.unique&&idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column().is_some_and(|n|t.columns.get(n).is_some_and(C::is_optional)))&&key.contains(&Value::Null){return Ok(false);}
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|live(t,r)).any(|r|idx.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(c,v)|c.column().is_some_and(|n|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v)))&&equalities.iter().all(|(n,v)|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v))))
    }
    fn created(&mut self,_:&str,_:&str,_:&[Value])->bool{false}

}
fn live(t:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,row:&Value)->bool{
    !t.expired(row,NOW)&&t.not_before.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|match row.field(c){Ok(Value::Int(n))=>*n<=NOW,Ok(Value::Null)=>true,_=>false})
}
fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{

    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};

    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    if p.has_reference(){return Err("reference intent requires authorized engine policy replay".into());}
    rules::evaluate(p,viewer,old,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn policy_in(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<bool,String>{
    let Some(p)=&inv.policy else{return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);};
    if !p.has_reference(){return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);}
    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    match engine.fixture_policy(p,viewer,old,&image,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?{
        rules::Verdict::Readable=>Ok(true),rules::Verdict::Denied=>Ok(false),rules::Verdict::Unknown=>Err("policy reference/coverage judgment is unknown; never a refutation".into()),
    }
}
fn program(name:&str,kind:ProgramKind,body:Vec<Stmt>)->Program{Program{name:name.into(),kind,args:BTreeMap::new(),body,read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false}}
fn engine(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probe:Program)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_with(bound,rows,vec![probe])
}
fn engine_with(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_on_store(bound,rows,probes,Store::memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)
}
fn hydrate(engine:&mut Engine,kind:&C,value:&Value)->Result<Value,String>{
        match (kind,value) {
            (C::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>hydrate(engine,inner,v),
            (C::List{of,..}|C::ListRange{of,..},Value::Array(xs))=>xs.iter().map(|v|hydrate(engine,of,v)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map(Value::Array),
            (C::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>engine.store_asset(VIEWER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|format!("verified image fixture: {e}")),
            (C::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(engine,VIEWER,types),
            _=>Ok(value.clone()),
        }
    }

fn engine_on_store(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>,store:Store)->Result<Engine,String>{
    let mut programs=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();programs.extend(probes);
    let mut engine=Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),programs,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let mut seeded=rows.iter().filter(|(name,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_none()).map(|(name,rs)|{
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
        let clean=rs.iter().map(|r|if let Value::Row(fields)=r {Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect())}else{r.clone()}).collect();
    if discharged(bound,inv){return;}
    if coupled(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: alternate value may break authored creator/guard coupling; only actual typed payloads and committed histories count",inv.target_label()));return;}
    if !reserve(out,1){return;}
    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
    let kind=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns[column];
    let mut other=match sample_rows(bound,rows,inv,row,presence){
        Some(other)=>other,
        None if kind.contains_asset()&&fixture.is_some()=>{
            let mut other=rows.clone();
            let Some(value)=sample(kind.required(),4)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media type unsupported",inv.table,column));return;};
            if let Some(Value::Row(fields))=other.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())){fields.insert(column.into(),value);}other
        },
        None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column pair needs a second typed value and, for media, a coupled verified fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;},
    };
    // Both worlds receive the same verified ledger, including the alternate
    // upload. Only the protected row value changes; no fabricated media ids.
    let ledger;
    let fixture=if kind.contains_asset(){
        let Some(base)=fixture else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: media column pair requires the fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;};
        let mut prepared=match fork(bound,base,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
        let target=other.get_mut(&inv.table).unwrap().iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
        if let Value::Row(fields)=target {
            let value=match hydrate(&mut prepared,kind,&fields[column]){Ok(v)=>v,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media upload: {why}",inv.table,column));return;}};
            fields.insert(column.into(),value);
        }
        ledger=prepared;Some(&ledger)
    }else{fixture};
    let altrow=other[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
    let observing=histories::observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
    let mut actors=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for r in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=r.field(c)&&!actors.contains(p){actors.push(p.clone());}}}}
    'actors: for viewer in &actors{
        if fixture.is_some()&&out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&histories::trace_key(&v.history)==histories::trace_key(trace.map(|(_,s)|s).unwrap_or(&[]))){continue;}

        {
            if !reserve(out,2){return;}
            let judge=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,rows,program("$column.intent",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))};
            let Ok(mut judge)=judge else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: primary column intent world unavailable",inv.target_label()));continue;};
            let actual=match readback(&judge){Ok(rows)=>rows,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}};
            let Some(primary)=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())else{continue;};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,primary,&mut judge)!=Ok(false){continue;}
        }
        for bp in &bound.programs{
            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
            let p=&bp.program;
            if ephemeral_operation(bound,p,out){continue;}
            let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
            if !observing.iter().any(|table|super::super::audit::mentions(&json,table)){continue;}
            if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native{continue;}
            let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
            if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|s|json.contains(s)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque column observer declined",p.name));continue;}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation {
                if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation has unknown policy dependencies; disclosure-boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(|r|stability::dependencies(bound,r)).unwrap_or_default();
                if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){
                    out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation can change protected row/policy before disclosure; boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;
                }
                if stability::implicit(bound,p).iter().any(|t|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: implicit writes can change column policy before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
            }
            let args_list=histories::fixture_cases(p,rows,&actors,out);
            for args in args_list{
                if !reserve(out,12){return;}
                let execute=|alternate:bool|->Result<(serde_json::Value,BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,BTreeMap<String,Value>),String>{
                    let seeds=if alternate{&other}else{rows};
                    let mut e=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{
                        let mut e=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())?;
                        if alternate{
                            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            let Value::Row(fields)=altrow else{return Err("not a row".into());};
                            let clean=Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect());
                            if let Err(why)=e.seed_at([(inv.table.clone(),clean.clone())],NOW){
                                let immutable=t.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|fields.contains(c)))||matches!(c,Constraint::AppendOnly))||matches!(t.columns[column].required(),C::Image{once:true,..});
                                if !immutable{return Err(why.to_string());}
                                if fixture.ephemeral_hub().snapshot().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.version.epoch!=0{return Err("immutable column alternate would drop existing ephemeral state; reduction uncovered".into());}
                                e=engine_on_store(bound,&other,Vec::new(),fixture.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
                            }
                        }e
                    }else{engine(bound,seeds,program("$column.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?};
                    validate_fixture(bound,&mut e)?;
                    let actual=readback(&e)?;
                    let current=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).ok_or("column target is absent after validation")?;
                    if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,current,&mut e)!=Ok(false){return Err("validated column-world policy is not false".into());}
                    let actual_args=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,&args,viewer)?;
                    let observed=paired::observe(&mut e,p,viewer,actual_args.clone())?;
                    Ok((observed,actual,actual_args))
                };
                let (a,primary_rows,primary_args,b,alternate_rows,alternate_args)=match (execute(false),execute(true)){
                    (Ok((a,primary,aa)),Ok((b,alternate,ba)))=>(a,primary,aa,b,alternate,ba),
                    (Err(why),_)|(_,Err(why))=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{} / {}: declined column world: {why}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),p.name));continue;}
                };
                out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                if primary_args!=alternate_args{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired column arguments require a coupled asset ledger; differing materialized inputs declined",p.name));continue;}
                let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
                if !same_base_world(bound,inv,&primary_rows,&alternate_rows,key){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: committed worlds change non-protected base fields/captures or populations; reduction unavailable",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));continue;}
                if a==b{continue;}
                if !matches!((execute(false),execute(true)),(Ok((aa,pa,aa_args)),Ok((bb,pb,bb_args))) if aa==a&&bb==b&&pa==primary_rows&&pb==alternate_rows&&aa_args==primary_args&&bb_args==alternate_args){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: repeated column inputs/worlds/output are unstable or unavailable",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
                let actual_row=primary_rows[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(key)).unwrap();
                let actual_alternate=alternate_rows[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(key)).unwrap();
                out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:if fixture.is_some(){Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace}else{Reachability::SyntheticOperation},invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:primary_args,viewer:viewer.clone(),operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"column read observation".into(),row:actual_row.clone(),old:Some(actual_alternate.clone()),supporting_rows:trace.map(|(initial,_)|initial.clone()).unwrap_or_else(||primary_rows.clone()),history:trace.map(|(_,steps)|steps.to_vec()).unwrap_or_default(),rule_path:format!("authored observer depends on {}.{} while policy is false in both typed worlds",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()),scope:if fixture.is_some(){"primary observation executes on trusted fixture; alternate column is a dependency test, not an authored alternate-history claim"}else{"schema-valid synthetic worlds; authored observer, no application reachability claim"}.into(),observations:Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:alternate_rows})});
                continue 'actors;
            }
        }
    }
}
fn run_pair(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,out:&mut Campaign){
    run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,false,out);
    if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(c)).is_some_and(C::is_optional){run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,true,out);}
}
pub(super) fn generated(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))){
        if inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column clock pairs use the campaign clock only; other temporal ticks not covered",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));}
        for variant in 0..16{
            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
            let rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column snapshot: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let e=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$column.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column seed: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
            for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,None,None,out);}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){break;}
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn fixture(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(rows)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),None,out);}
    }
}

pub(super) fn after_history(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,steps:&[histories::Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    let rows=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){

        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),Some((initial,steps)),out);}
    }
}
