//! `check`'s advisory notes, ranked. A green check printed nine notes in
//! source order, and builders could not tell an FYI about offline coverage
//! from a page that never completes. Notes stay advisory (they never fail a
//! check); this only orders them, names each group, and shortens repeats.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum Tier {
    /// Can cost correctness or completeness: a page that never completes, a
    /// write no caller can pass, a disclosure, a read a device cannot answer.
    Review,
    /// Costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage.
    Performance,
    /// What a declaration implies; nothing to fix for online use.
    Design,
}

pub(crate) fn tier(code: &str) -> Tier {
    match code {
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" | "N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL" | "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" | "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" | "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" | "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" | "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" | "N_WRITE_DENIED" | "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" | "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" | "N_PATCH_CLEARS" | "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" | "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_VACUOUS_RULE" | "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" | "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" | "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" | "N_QUERY_FILTERED" | "N_SHAPE_METER" | "N_HELD_UNKNOWN" | "N_SEQUENCE_READ"
        | "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" | "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" | "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"
        | "N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED" | "N_SEARCH_OPEN" | "N_TIMELINE_PROFILE_CAP" | "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" | "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => Tier::Review,
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.4 — a cursorless `first N` is what the declaration says: the first N rows (wave 3 #7).
        "N_SYNC_ORDER" | "N_NO_HORIZON" | "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED" | "N_TIMELINE_COMPAT" | "N_NO_CURSOR" => Tier::Design,
        _ => Tier::Performance,
    }
}

/// Security findings that can grant authority stay ahead of repeated advice.
pub(crate) fn summary_order(code:&str)->(Tier,u8){
    (tier(code),match code{
        "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"=>0,"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"=>1,
        "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"=>3,"N_BROAD_READ"=>4,"N_SECURITY_INTENT"=>5,_=>2,
    })
}

/// The first words of a note's line: what to do about it, or that nothing
/// needs doing (wave 7 #11: "put a one-line verdict on every advisory note").
// @ref LLP 3000.036#17.7 — the verdict leads; the explanation follows it.
pub(crate) fn verdict(code: &str, summary: &str, form: bool) -> &'static str {
    match code {
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" if summary.starts_with("persona-only:") => "no action without use identity",
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" => "fix: add a production bootstrap writer",
        "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" => "fix: update the fixture arguments",
        "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" => "review: complete for the pinned parent audience; other callers may be capped",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("the complete form is to add `cap ") => "fix: add the exact source cap below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("no verified complete form for this read") => "review: no verified complete form for this read; expect `capped` and show what arrived",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form && summary.contains("the closest form is") => "fix: use the closest form below (one arm stays `complete: false`), or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form => "fix: use the complete form below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" => "fix: pin the scope, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" => "fix: declare the reference's `on delete`, or guard the delete",
        "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" => "review: some deleters get NOT_FOUND",
        "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" => "review: this reference argument has no mandatory target lookup",
        "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" => "review: hidden and absent are different caller outcomes",
        "N_PATCH_CLEARS" => "review: an omitted argument clears this column",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" if summary.contains("admits only 'service:") => "review: a seat require grants no table authority",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" => "fix: this write refuses E_RULE for callers you admit",
        "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" => "review: callers you admit may still get E_RULE",
        "N_QUERY_FILTERED" => "review: this query branch may filter rows under table authority",
        "N_READ_DENIED" => "fix: this read always returns null",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" if summary.contains("get NOT_FOUND") => "review: the hidden lookup runs before this require",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" => "fix: this require cannot see the rows it tests",
        "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" => "fix: this nullable lookup is always null for the admitted callers",
        "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" => "review: a required read gate may hide rows from writers",
        "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" => "review: who can read this",
        "N_VACUOUS_RULE" => "review: who can write this",
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" => "review: readable conflicts require the original immutable value",
        "N_NO_CURSOR" => "no action for a top-N list",
        _ => match tier(code) {
            Tier::Review => "review",
            Tier::Performance => "no action unless it is slow (`check --cost` measures it)",
            Tier::Design => "no action for an online app",
        },
    }
}

fn heading(tier: Tier) -> &'static str {
    match tier {
        Tier::Review => "review — can cost correctness or completeness:",
        Tier::Performance => "performance — costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage:",
        Tier::Design => "by design — what a declaration implies; no action needed for online use:",
    }
}

pub(crate) struct Note {
    pub site: String,
    pub code: String,
    pub summary: String,
    /// An expanded compiled repair: its lead line and the code. Compact
    /// completeness notes leave this empty and keep the repair in `--verbose`.
    pub form: Option<(String, String)>,
    /// Three-line default rendering for completeness verdicts. Their full
    /// planner proof and compiled repair remain in `check --verbose`.
    pub compact: Option<[String; 3]>,
}

/// The compiled form leading a note's rewrite: the text before "Otherwise:",
/// when it holds code (a lead line ending in `:` and the declaration under it).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.5 — the compiled owner-or-X rewrite prints by default, once per form.
pub(crate) fn compiled_form(code: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    if !matches!(code, "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE") { return None; }
    let head = rewrite.split("\n\nOtherwise:").next()?;
    let (lead, body) = head.split_once('\n')?;
    (lead.ends_with(':') && body.trim_start().starts_with("query ")).then(|| (lead.to_owned(), body.trim_end().to_owned()))
}

/// The ranked block printed after `check: ok`; empty for no notes.
pub(crate) fn render(notes: Vec<Note>) -> Vec<String> {
    if notes.is_empty() { return Vec::new(); }
    // Collapse one code's repeats at one site, keeping first-seen order.
    let mut unique: Vec<(Note, usize)> = Vec::new();
    for note in notes {
        match unique.iter_mut().find(|(seen, _)| seen.site == note.site && seen.code == note.code && seen.summary == note.summary && seen.form == note.form && seen.compact == note.compact) {
            Some((_, count)) => *count += 1,
            None => unique.push((note, 1)),
        }
    }
    let total = unique.len();
    // Rank by tier; within a tier, a code's notes stay together in the order the code first appeared.
    let first_seen = |code: &str, unique: &[(Note, usize)]| unique.iter().position(|(n, _)| n.code == code).unwrap();
    let mut order: Vec<usize> = (0..unique.len()).collect();
    order.sort_by_key(|&i| (tier(&unique[i].0.code), first_seen(&unique[i].0.code, &unique), i));
    let mut lines = vec![format!("{total} advisory note{} — notes never block dev, test or deploy (most consequential first; check --verbose explains each, snapback4 why <CODE> one):",
        if total == 1 { "" } else { "s" })];
    let mut current = None;
    let mut shown_codes = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_forms = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_compacts = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_actions = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_context = Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#16.5 — wave 6 #25: a code's repeats share one line naming each subject.
    let mut repeats: Vec<(String, Vec<String>)> = Vec::new();
    let flush = |lines: &mut Vec<String>, repeats: &mut Vec<(String, Vec<String>)>| {
        for (code, subjects) in repeats.drain(..) {
            lines.push(format!("note: {code} (as above) also for {}", subjects.join("; ")));
        }
    };
    for i in order {
        let (note, count) = &unique[i];
        let group = tier(&note.code);
        if current != Some(group) { flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats); lines.push(heading(group).to_owned()); current = Some(group); }
        if note.code == "N_NO_CURSOR" {
            if !shown_codes.contains(&note.code) {
                let subjects = unique.iter().filter(|(n,_)| n.code == "N_NO_CURSOR").map(|(n,_)|
                    format!("{} ({})", subject(&n.summary), n.site)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ");
                lines.push(format!("note: N_NO_CURSOR: no action for a top-N list. Pages without continuation: {subjects}; if the screen pages, add `c: cursor ?` and `after c`"));
                shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
            }
            continue;
        }
        let times = if *count > 1 { format!(" (×{count} at this site)") } else { String::new() };

exec
/bin/bash -lc "timeout 25s bash -c 'sed -n \"225,370p\" snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges.rs; cat snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/timed.rs; sed -n \"95,190p\" snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/histories.rs; cat snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/proof_source.rs'" in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4
 succeeded in 0ms:
        !hits.is_empty()&&!(dep==&inv.table&&hits.len()==1&&hits[0].1=="insert")
    });
    if unstable||!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty()||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.maintained.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())){
        return Err("operation policy dependency may change before/after its write boundary; snapshot correspondence unproved".into());
    }Ok(())
}

/// Schema-only engine challenges. Successful synthetic operations refute the
/// declared table-authority contract, not application-operation reachability.
pub fn run(bound:&Bound)->Campaign{run_selected(bound,None)}
/// `check` searches unproved direct obligations; unsearched blockers remain
/// explicitly unproved. Default `test` passes None and runs the whole set.
pub fn run_selected(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>)->Campaign{run_selected_after(bound,sites,Campaign::default())}
/// Continue after trusted observations, preserving one set of work budgets.
pub fn run_selected_after(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>,mut out:Campaign)->Campaign{
    // A direct component theorem needs no repeated concrete search for that
    // same component. Indirect observers, temporal reads and actual authored
    // operations below still run; the whole declaration remains unproved.
    let discharged=super::analyze(bound).invariants.into_iter()
        .filter(|v|v.direct_rule==Status::Proved&&v.invariant.operation.is_none()
            && (matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"|"insert"|"delete")
                || matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"update"|"write")&&bound.schema.tables[&v.invariant.table].written_by.is_empty()))
        .map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut assertions=bound.invariants.clone();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
        if inv.operation.is_some()||matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"){continue;}
        for p in &bound.programs {if p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
            let relevant=ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table&&(inv.target=="write"||inv.target==*kind||*kind=="upsert"));
            if relevant{let mut op=inv.clone();op.operation=Some(p.program.name.clone());assertions.push(op);}
        }
    }
    for inv in &assertions{
        if out.exhausted(){break;}
        if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.reachability.is_reachable()){continue;}
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
        if sites.is_some_and(|sites|!sites.contains(&inv.site.to_string())){continue;}
        if inv.operation.is_none()&&discharged.contains(&inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
        if let Err(why)=operation_preflight(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}
        let mut found=false;let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
        let combinations=bound.schema.tables.values().flat_map(|t|t.columns.values()).filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).fold(1usize,usize::saturating_mul).max(24);
        if combinations>512{out.unsupported.insert("schema enum Cartesian matrix exceeds 512 samples; remaining tuples are not covered".into());}
        for sample in 0..=combinations.min(512) {
            if !out.reserve(1){break;}
            let variant=sample.saturating_sub(1);
            let mut rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            // Try explicit policy field literals before the unchanged enum
            // matrix. A value such as status='cancelled' need not wait behind
            // hundreds of unrelated enum tuples. This is only a candidate;
            // ordinary validation and engine replay still decide refutation.
            if sample==0 {
                fn pin(rule:&RuleExpr,row:&mut Value)->bool{match rule{
                    RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(v))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(v))|
                    RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Field(f))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Field(f))=>{
                        if let Value::Row(fields)=row&&fields.contains_key(f){fields.insert(f.clone(),v.clone());true}else{false}
                    },
                    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().fold(false,|any,r|pin(r,row)||any),
                    RuleExpr::Not(r)=>pin(r,row),_=>false,
                }}
                if !inv.policy.as_ref().zip(rows.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first_mut())).is_some_and(|(p,row)|pin(p,row)){continue;}
            }
            let candidate_args=inv.operation.as_ref().and_then(|op|bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op)).and_then(|p|arguments(&p.program.args,variant));
            // Pruned dependencies can repeat after unrelated schema enum digits.
            // Identical seed/typed-argument candidates are one search input.
            if !seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&rows,&candidate_args)).unwrap()){continue;}
            let mut seeded=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(Read{site:"security read".into(),table:inv.table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(id(&inv.table))],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed}))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
            let rows=match readback(&seeded){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
            let Some(row)=rows.get(&inv.table).and_then(|r|r.first())else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no generated ordinary row",inv.table));break;};
            for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER,"account:security-no-role","service:security"]{
                if !out.reserve(1){break;}
                if inv.target=="always" {
                    match policy(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,None,row){
                        Ok(false)=>{out.attempted+=1;out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::TrustedSeedOnly,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:"$security.seed_at".into(),effect:"always".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:rows.clone(),rule_path:"trusted seed ingress; all typed and final-exists constraints rechecked".into(),scope:"actual seed_at commit/read-back; ordinary application reachability is not claimed".into()});found=true;},
                        Ok(true)=>{out.attempted+=1;},Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);}
                    }if found{break;}continue;
                }
                if let Some(operation)=&inv.operation {
                    let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==operation)else{continue;};
                    let permitted=match policy(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,None,row){Ok(p)=>p,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{operation}: operation intent not judged: {why}"));continue;}};
                    let Some(args)=arguments(&p.program.args,variant)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{operation}: arguments need fixture assets or unsupported samples"));continue;};
                    if !out.reserve(1){break;}
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    let mut seeded=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut seeded,&p.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{operation}: argument ledger unavailable: {why}"));continue;}};
                    match seeded.mutate(operation,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)) {
                        Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})=>{
                            let current=readback(&seeded).unwrap_or_default();
                            let changed=current.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|!rows.get(&inv.table).is_some_and(|old|old.contains(r))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                            for next in changed {
                                let old=rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|old|old.field("id").ok()==next.field("id").ok());
                                // An upsert must be attributed to its executed arm;
                                // a column contract cannot be refuted by editing a sibling.
                                if inv.target=="update"&&old.is_none() || inv.target=="insert"&&old.is_some() || inv.target=="delete" {continue;}
                                if let Some(column)=&inv.column && old.is_some_and(|old|old.field(column).ok()==next.field(column).ok()){continue;}
                                if !permitted{out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:operation.clone(),effect:if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"}.into(),row:next.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:rows.clone(),rule_path:format!("authored operation {operation}"),scope:"actual authored mutation committed a changed image from a schema-valid seeded snapshot".into()});found=true;break;}
                            }
                        },_=>out.refused+=1,
                    }
                    if found{break;}continue;
                }
                for word in super::effects(inv){
                    let mut admission_rows=rows.clone();if word=="insert"{admission_rows.remove(&inv.table);}
                    let judged=if word=="read"{policy_in(bound,inv,&admission_rows,viewer,Some(row),row,&mut seeded)}else{policy(bound,inv,&admission_rows,viewer,(word!="insert").then_some(row),row)};
                    let allowed=match judged{Ok(v)=>v,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                    if allowed{continue;}
                    if !out.reserve(1){break;}
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    let replayed=if word=="read" {seeded.query("$security.seed",Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string())}else{replay(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,word,row)};
                    match replayed{
                        Ok((false,_))=>out.refused+=1,
                        Ok((true,next))=>{
                            let mut args=BTreeMap::new();
                            if word=="update"{let changes=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.keys().filter(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()).filter_map(|c|next.field(c).ok().map(|v|(c.clone(),v.clone()))).collect();args.insert("$syntheticPatch".into(),Value::Map(changes));}
                            out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args,viewer:viewer.into(),operation:format!("$security.{}.{}",inv.table,word),effect:word.into(),row:next,old:(word=="update"||word=="delete").then(||row.clone()),supporting_rows:admission_rows.clone(),rule_path:bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].rules.for_effect(super::effect(word)).quarry(),scope:if word=="update"{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; old-image policy false at an actual changed update; committed next image and patch shown; application reachability is not claimed".into()}else{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; synthetic engine table operation; application reachability is not claimed".into()}});found=true;break;},
                        Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: {e}",inv.table,word));},
                    }
                }
                if found{break;}
            }
            if found{break;}
        }
    }
    column_reads::generated(bound,&mut out,sites);
    paired::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    timed::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    out.unsupported.insert("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require a live server; engine principals are authenticated contexts".into());
    out.unsupported.insert("offline/revoked-device access and retained history require device boundary qualification".into());out
}
pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){
    report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());
    let rank=|c:&Counterexample|match c.reachability{Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace=>0,Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace=>1,_=>2};
    let committed=|c:&Counterexample|c.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    for c in &campaign.violations {for v in &mut report.invariants {if v.invariant.site.to_string()!=c.invariant_site{continue;}
        if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|rank(old)<rank(c)||rank(old)==rank(c)&&committed(old)<=committed(c)){continue;}
        v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());
        v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; fixture origin is explicit, not an empty-state reachability theorem".into());
    }}
}

fn arguments(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,variant:usize)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    use ir::ArgType as A;
    fn one(k:&A,v:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match k{
        A::Ref{table}=>id(table),A::Principal=>Value::Id(if v&1==0{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),A::Id=>Value::Id("security-argument".into()),A::Cursor|A::Optional(_)=>Value::Null,
        A::Text{min,max,..}=>Value::String("z".repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize)),A::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(v%variants.len())?.clone()),A::Int|A::Decimal{..}|A::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),A::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),A::Time=>Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000),A::Bool=>Value::Bool(v&2==0),A::List{of:Some(inner),..}=>Value::Array(vec![one(inner,v)?]),A::ListRange{of:Some(inner),min,..}=>Value::Array((0..*min).map(|_|one(inner,v)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),A::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),A::Json{..}=>Value::Null,A::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),A::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),A::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),_=>return None,
    })}
    args.iter().map(|(n,k)|one(k,variant).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
//! Exercise actual logical lifetime reads between deadlines and cleanup ticks.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — no snapshot-only clock refutation.
use super::*;

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock)
        && sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
        let policy=inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap();
        if policy.has_reference(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: temporal reference policy needs authorized per-tick engine replay",inv.table));continue;}
        if !kernel::dependencies(policy).is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: clock policy with raw-probe dependencies needs a live-snapshot evaluator",inv.table));continue;}
        let name="$security.temporal";
        let probe=program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(Read{site:"temporal security".into(),table:inv.table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),
            key:vec![Expr::Literal(id(&inv.table))],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed}))]);
        for variant in [0,4] {
            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
            let rows=match snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,true){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
            for now in [NOW,NOW+999,NOW+1000,NOW+1001,NOW+1999,NOW+2000,NOW+2001] {
                if !out.reserve(1){return;}
                // Recreate the store for each boundary; never sweep before the
                // read. Unswept expiry remains a logical absence by contract.
                let mut engine=match engine(bound,&rows,probe.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                let actual=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                let Some(row)=actual.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())else{continue;};
                for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER] {
                    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    let Ok(q)=engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(now))else{continue;};
                    if q.unavailable||!q.complete||q.data==Value::Null{continue;}
                    // Scalar clock atoms are evaluated only after a real
                    // engine read returned this committed row. This extra
                    // field is proof-private and never enters the store.
                    let mut image=row.clone();
                    if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image {fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(now));}
                    if matches!(rules::evaluate(policy,viewer,None,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows:&actual}),Ok(false)) {
                        out.admitted+=1;
                        out.violations.push(Counterexample {reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:name.into(),
                            effect:"read".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:actual.clone(),rule_path:format!("server read at {now}, before any physical sweep; clock policy false"),
                            scope:"actual engine read over schema-valid committed seed ingress at the recorded server clock; schedules are not assumed to have run".into()});
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if queries.is_empty(){return;}
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            if out.exhausted(){return;}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                        if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
                        continue 'actors;
                    }
                }
            }
            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
            if !reserve_observers(out,3){return;}
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world invalid: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|observing.iter().any(|t|super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t))){
                let p=&bp.program;
                let cases=fixture_cases(p,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,4){return;}
                    let raw_args=args;
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&p.args,&raw_args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                    if args!=raw_args{
                        if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                        absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger fork: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                        if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger absence: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: ledger absence world invalid: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if readback(&absent)!=Ok(absent_rows.clone()){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired argument ledger changes the absent base world",p.name));continue;}
                    }
                    out.attempted+=2;
                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
                    if a==b{continue;}
                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: paired history observation is unstable or unavailable on repeat",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
                    let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
                    if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
                    continue 'actors;
                }
            }
        }}
    }
}
fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
}
fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — explicit saved bytes replace only source selection, never activation or store semantics.
use super::{Options, Project};
use std::path::Path;

/// Select one complete saved backend, or the ordinary multi-file project.
/// The vector is retained for both compilation and staging: one read, no swap.
pub(super) fn sources(project: &Project, options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, String> {
    match options.flags.get(flag) {
        None => project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        Some(file) => {
            let path = Path::new(file);
            if !path.try_exists().map_err(|e|format!("cannot inspect saved source {file}: {e}"))? {return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist"));}
            if path.is_dir() {
                return crate::client_compatibility::sources(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
            }
            let name = path.file_name().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).ok_or("saved source needs a UTF-8 file name")?;
            if !name.ends_with(".q") { return Err("saved source must be a complete Quarry .q file".into()); }
            let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound {format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist")} else {format!("cannot read saved source {file}: {e}")})?;
            if text.trim().is_empty() { return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved Quarry source is empty")); }
            // A saved complete backend retains the schema namespace regardless of its archive file name.
            Ok(vec![("schema.q".into(), text)])
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn compile(sources: &[(String, String)], options: &Options, flag: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let mut bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get(flag) {
        for diagnostic in &mut bound.diagnostics { diagnostic.site.file = if Path::new(file).is_dir() { Path::new(file).join(&diagnostic.site.file).to_string_lossy().into_owned() } else { file.clone() }; }
    }
    bound
}

/// A saved file can be used outside a project. Companion code is selected
/// only from an actual current project, never from arbitrary sibling files.
pub(super) fn project(options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Project, String> {
    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if let Some(project) = Project::locate(&cwd) { return Ok(project); }
    if options.flags.contains_key(flag) { return Ok(Project { root: cwd }); }
    Err("no snapback/*.q here or above; run `snapback4 init` or select a saved source file".into())
}

/// A private companion build uses the selected schema's generated contracts.
/// Packaging still records the original host installation, never this scratch.
pub(super) struct Candidate { pub project: Project, cleanup: std::path::PathBuf }
impl Drop for Candidate { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.cleanup); } }
impl Candidate {
    pub(super) fn prepare(original: &Project, stage: &Path, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound) -> Result<Self, String> {
        use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
        static SERIAL: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-saved-source-{}-{}", std::process::id(), SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        #[cfg(unix)] snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut candidate = Self { project: Project { root: root.clone() }, cleanup: root };
        candidate.project.root = crate::dev_worker::private_scope(original, &candidate.cleanup)?;
        // The worker snapshotter captures the actual import closure and refuses
        // symlinks. Copy those captured bytes, not an unbounded application tree.
        copy_tree(stage, &candidate.project.root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        // Discover the current dependency graph without installing selected
        // contracts in the working project. A failed typecheck still publishes
        // its graph; the copied bytes are checked with selected contracts below.
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(original) {
            let _ = original.check_entry(&entry);
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        }
        candidate.project.write_generated_from(bound, original).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if !crate::native::declared(bound).is_empty() && original.native_manifest().exists() {
            copy_tree(&original.native_dir(), &candidate.project.native_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            rebase_tree(&candidate.project.native_dir(), &original.native_dir()).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native snapshot: {e}"))?;
        }
        crate::dev_worker::link_dependencies(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(&candidate.project) { candidate.project.check_entry(&entry)?; }
        Ok(candidate)
    }
}
fn copy_tree(source: &Path, target: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(target)?;
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(source)? {
        let entry = entry?;
        if matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(), Some("target" | "native-target" | ".git")) { continue; }
        let kind = entry.file_type()?;
        if kind.is_dir() { copy_tree(&entry.path(), &target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else if kind.is_file() { std::fs::copy(entry.path(), target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else { return Err(std::io::Error::other("saved-source companion snapshot permits regular source files only")); }
    }
    Ok(())
}


#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn dir(label: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-saved-{}-{label}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root); std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback/generated")).unwrap(); root
    }
    #[test]
    fn archived_names_preserve_operations_and_diagnostics_name_the_selected_input() {
        let root = dir("archived"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        let file = root.join("v1.q");
        std::fs::write(&file, "query alive():\n  return true\n").unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], flags: [("schema".into(), file.to_str().unwrap().into())].into(), json: false };
        let selected = sources(&project, &options, "schema").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(selected[0].0, "schema.q");
        let bound = compile(&selected, &options, "schema");
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty()); assert_eq!(bound.programs[0].program.name, "alive");
        std::fs::write(&file, "not a Quarry backend").unwrap();
        let bound = compile(&sources(&project, &options, "schema").unwrap(), &options, "schema");
        assert!(!bound.diagnostics.is_empty()); assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().all(|d| d.site.file == file.to_str().unwrap()));
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn missing_baseline_names_original_absolute_location() {
        let root = dir("missing-baseline"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::write(root.join("snapback/client-compatibility.json"), r#"{"baseline":"versions/v1","since_release":2}"#).unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "query alive():\n  return true\n".into())]);
        let error = project.write_generated(&bound).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(error.contains(root.join("versions/v1").to_str().unwrap()) && error.contains("does not exist (resolved from") && error.contains("snapback/client-compatibility.json"), "{error}");
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn inherited_native_workspace_refuses_before_touching_working_generated_types() {
        let root = dir("workspace"); let stage = dir("workspace-stage");
        let original = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::create_dir_all(original.native_dir()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), "[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion.workspace = true\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_types(), "working sentinel").unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "job sweep(): native\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let error = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).err().unwrap();
        assert!(error.contains("workspace-inherited") && error.contains("standalone"), "{error}");
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap(); std::fs::remove_dir_all(stage).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn native_types_are_built_for_selected_source_in_private_tree() {
        let root = dir("native"); let stage = dir("stage");
        let original = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::create_dir_all(original.native_dir().join("src")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), "[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion = \"0.0.0\"\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_types(), "working sentinel").unwrap();
        let source = "table notes:\n  title: text <=80\n  color: text <=20\n  public 'shared'\njob sweep(): native\n";
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("v2.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let candidate = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).unwrap();
        crate::native::build(&candidate.project, &bound).err().expect("private build refuses absent native target"); // no lib target: a private build refusal, never working generated writes
        assert!(std::fs::read_to_string(candidate.project.native_types()).unwrap().contains("color"));
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        let manifest = "[dependencies]\nsdk = { path = \"../sdk\" }\n";
        std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback/sdk")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), manifest).unwrap();
        let relocated = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).unwrap();
        let parsed: toml::Value = std::fs::read_to_string(relocated.project.native_manifest()).unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(parsed["dependencies"]["sdk"]["path"].as_str().unwrap(), root.join("snapback/sdk").canonicalize().unwrap().to_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_manifest()).unwrap(), manifest);
        drop(relocated);
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        drop(candidate); std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap(); std::fs::remove_dir_all(stage).unwrap();
    }
}

fn rebase_native(text: &str, original: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path, workspace: bool) -> Result<String, String> {
    let mut manifest: toml::Value = text.parse().map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native manifest: {e}"))?;
    fn inherited(value: &toml::Value) -> bool {
        let marker = |field: &toml::Value| field.get("workspace").and_then(toml::Value::as_bool) == Some(true);
        if value.get("package").and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|fields| fields.iter().any(|(key, field)| key != "metadata" && marker(field))) { return true; }
        for key in ["dependencies", "dev-dependencies", "build-dependencies"] {
            if value.get(key).and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|fields| fields.values().any(marker)) { return true; }
        }
        value.get("target").and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|targets| targets.values().any(inherited))
    }
    if !workspace && inherited(&manifest) { return Err("workspace-inherited native packages require a complete standalone [workspace] under snapback/native; copy its workspace package/dependency declarations before saved-source deployment".into()); }
    fn dependency_group(group: &mut toml::Value, root: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        if let Some(dependencies) = group.as_table_mut() {
            for (_, dependency) in dependencies.iter_mut() {
                if let Some(path) = dependency.as_table_mut().and_then(|fields| fields.get_mut("path")) {
                    let relative = path.as_str().ok_or("native dependency path must be a string")?;
                    let absolute = root.join(relative).canonicalize().map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native dependency {relative}: {e}"))?;
                    let absolute = absolute.strip_prefix(source_root).map(|relative| target_root.join(relative)).unwrap_or(absolute.clone());
                    *path = toml::Value::String(absolute.to_str().ok_or("native dependency path must be UTF-8")?.to_owned());
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn groups(table: &mut toml::Value, root: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        for key in ["dependencies", "dev-dependencies", "build-dependencies", "replace"] {
            if let Some(group) = table.get_mut(key) { dependency_group(group, root, source_root, target_root)?; }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    groups(&mut manifest, original, source_root, target_root)?;
    for category in ["target", "patch"] {
        if let Some(table) = manifest.get_mut(category).and_then(toml::Value::as_table_mut) {
            for (_, item) in table.iter_mut() {
                if category == "target" { groups(item, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
                else { dependency_group(item, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
            }
        }
    }
    if let Some(workspace) = manifest.get_mut("workspace") { groups(workspace, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
    toml::to_string(&manifest).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native manifest: {e}"))
}

fn rebase_tree(target: &Path, original: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(target.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let manifest: toml::Value = text.parse().map_err(|e| format!("native manifest: {e}"))?;
    let workspace = manifest.get("workspace").is_some();
    fn walk(directory: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path, workspace: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(directory).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            let entry = entry.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if entry.file_type().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_dir() { walk(&entry.path(), source_root, target_root, workspace)?; }
            else if entry.file_name() == "Cargo.toml" {
                let relative = entry.path().strip_prefix(target_root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.to_owned();
                let source = source_root.join(relative).parent().unwrap().to_owned();
                let text = std::fs::read_to_string(entry.path()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                let rebased = rebase_native(&text, &source, source_root, target_root, workspace)?;
                std::fs::write(entry.path(), rebased).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    let original = original.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    walk(target, &original, target, workspace)
}

    if word=="update" {

    Ok((true,next))
}
fn readback(engine:&Engine)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{engine.schema().tables.keys().map(|t|engine.store().rows(t).map(|rs|(t.clone(),rs)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).collect()}

fn operation_preflight(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<(),String>{
    let Some(name)=&inv.operation else{return Ok(());};
    let p=&bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==name).ok_or("operation did not bind")?.program;
    if !super::census::body(&p.body){return Err(format!("{name}: opaque/loop/scan path requires write-boundary tracing"));}
    let rule=inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("operation policy outside rule fragment")?;
    if rule.any_term(&|t|!matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))){return Err("operation policy requires write-boundary row-image tracing".into());}
    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
    if writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).count()>1{return Err(format!("{name}: multiple target writes need executed-effect tracing; net images do not identify delete/reinsert"));}
    let unstable=kernel::dependencies(rule).iter().any(|dep|{
        let hits=writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==dep).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        !hits.is_empty()&&!(dep==&inv.table&&hits.len()==1&&hits[0].1=="insert")
    });
    if unstable||!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty()||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.maintained.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())){
        return Err("operation policy dependency may change before/after its write boundary; snapshot correspondence unproved".into());
    }Ok(())
}

/// Schema-only engine challenges. Successful synthetic operations refute the
/// declared table-authority contract, not application-operation reachability.
pub fn run(bound:&Bound)->Campaign{run_selected(bound,None)}
/// `check` searches unproved direct obligations; unsearched blockers remain
//! Column worlds execute authored observers, including derived/count/refusal paths.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#121-column-read-intent — excluding a field from a projection must not produce a synthetic whole-row refutation.
use super::*;
fn reserve(out:&mut Campaign,cost:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(cost){return false;}
    if out.column_observer_calls+cost>16384{out.unsupported.insert("column observers capped at 16384 call/world reservations; remaining value worlds not covered".into());return false;}
    out.column_observer_calls+=cost;true
}
fn discharged(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->bool{
    inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true))
        &&!bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)
        &&!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)))
        &&!bound.programs.iter().any(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(t,_)|t!=&inv.table)&&super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&inv.table))
}
fn alternative(kind:&C,old:&Value)->Option<Value>{Some(match (kind.required(),old){
    (C::Text{max},Value::String(s)) if s.is_empty()=>if *max>0{Value::String("x".into())}else{return None},
    (C::Text{..},Value::String(s))=>Value::String(s.chars().map(|c|if c=='y'{'x'}else{'y'}).collect()),
    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|s|Value::String((*s).clone())!=*old)?.clone()),
    (C::Principal,Value::Id(s))=>Value::Id(if s==VIEWER{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),
    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(n.checked_add(1)?),
    (C::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(if n<max{n+1}else if n>min{n-1}else{return None}),
    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
    _=>return None,
})}
fn sample_rows(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,inv:&Invariant,row:&Value,presence:bool)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>{
    let column=inv.column.as_ref()?;let kind=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(column)?;
    let old=match row.field(column){
        Ok(value)=>value.clone(),
        Err(_) if kind.is_optional()&&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].defaults.get(column).is_none_or(|d|matches!(d,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(Value::Null)))=>Value::Null,
        Err(_)=>return None,
    };
    let alt=if presence&&kind.is_optional(){if old==Value::Null{sample(kind.required(),0)?}else{Value::Null}}else{alternative(kind,&old)?};
    let mut out=rows.clone();
    let selected=out.get_mut(&inv.table)?.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())?;
    if let Value::Row(fields)=selected{fields.insert(column.clone(),alt);}else{return None;}
    Some(out)
}
fn same_base_world(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,primary:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,alternate:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,key:&Value)->bool{
    bound.schema.tables.iter().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).all(|(table,definition)|{
        let empty=Vec::new();let rows=primary.get(table).unwrap_or(&empty);let other=alternate.get(table).unwrap_or(&empty);
        rows.len()==other.len()&&rows.iter().all(|old|other.iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|{
            let (Value::Row(a),Value::Row(b))=(old,new)else{return false;};
            a.keys().chain(b.keys()).filter(|c|!definition.maintained.contains_key(*c)&&!(table==&inv.table&&inv.column.as_ref()==Some(*c)&&old.field("id").ok()==Some(key))).all(|c|old.field(c).ok()==new.field(c).ok())
        }))
    })
}
// Counterfactual field mutation must not break an authored creator's copies
// or recipient guards. Direct typed payload observations run independently.
fn coupled(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->bool{
    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
    let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    if table.rules.insert.needs_field_value(column,&table.columns[column])||table.update_rule_judged().needs_field_value(column,&table.columns[column]){return true;}
    fn definitions(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>){
        if let Some(object)=value.as_object(){
            for tag in ["Const","Let","Assign"]{if let Some(binding)=object.get(tag)&&let (Some(name),Some(value))=(binding.get("name").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binding.get("value")){
                if tag=="Assign"||out.contains_key(name){out.insert(name.into(),serde_json::Value::Null);}else{out.insert(name.into(),value.clone());}
            }}
            for child in object.values(){definitions(child,out);}
        }else if let Some(xs)=value.as_array(){for child in xs{definitions(child,out);}}
    }
    fn fields<'a>(expression:&'a serde_json::Value,bindings:&'a BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,depth:usize)->Option<&'a serde_json::Map<String,serde_json::Value>>{
        if depth>32{return None;}
        if let Some(map)=expression.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return Some(map);}
        if let Some(projection)=expression.get("Builtin")&&projection.get("builtin").is_some_and(|b|b.get("ProjectFields").is_some()){
            return fields(projection.get("args")?.as_array()?.first()?,bindings,depth+1);
        }
        if let Some(name)=expression.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str){return fields(bindings.get(name)?,bindings,depth+1);}
        None
    }
    fn dependency(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,depth:usize)->bool{
        if depth>32{return true;}
        if let Some(field)=value.get("Field")&&let (Some(base),Some(name))=(field.get("base"),field.get("name").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str))&&let Some(map)=fields(base,bindings,depth+1){
            return map.get(name).is_none_or(|expression|dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1));
        }
        if let Some(name)=value.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str){
            if name==arg{return true;}
            if let Some(bound)=bindings.get(name){return bound.is_null()||dependency(bound,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1);}
            return !args.contains_key(name);
        }
        // A guard re-reading the written table may inspect the supplied field
        // through a row alias even when the argument itself occurs only once.
        for tag in ["Get","Exists"]{if value.get(tag).and_then(|v|v.get("table")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(table){return true;}}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|dependency(v,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|dependency(v,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1)),_=>false}
    }
    fn guarded(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>)->bool{
        for tag in ["Require","Check","If"]{if let Some(condition)=value.get(tag).and_then(|v|v.get("condition"))&&dependency(condition,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}}
        for tag in ["Binary","Unary","Builtin","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"]{if let Some(expression)=value.get(tag){
            if tag=="Builtin"&&expression.get("builtin").is_some_and(|b|b.get("ProjectFields").is_some()){continue;}
            if dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}
        }}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|guarded(v,arg,table,args,bindings)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|guarded(v,arg,table,args,bindings)),_=>false}
    }
    fn related_write(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,column:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>)->bool{
        for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
            let Some(write)=value.get(tag)else{continue;};
            let name=write.get("table").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
            if let Some(row)=write.get("row").or_else(||write.get("patch")){
                if let Some(map)=fields(row,bindings,0){
                    if map.iter().any(|(field,expression)|(name!=Some(table)||field!=column)&&dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,0)){return true;}
                }else if dependency(row,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}
            }
            if write.get("key").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|target|target.get("key"))).is_some_and(|key|dependency(key,arg,table,args,bindings,0))||write.get("condition").is_some_and(|condition|dependency(condition,arg,table,args,bindings,0)){return true;}
        }
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|related_write(v,arg,table,column,args,bindings)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|related_write(v,arg,table,column,args,bindings)),_=>false}
    }
    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,table:&str,column:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,program:&str,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,guards:&BTreeSet<String>,defaulted:bool,found:&mut bool,coupled:&mut bool){
        if let Some(object)=value.as_object(){
            for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
                let Some(write)=object.get(tag)else{continue;};
                let name=write.get("table").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
                if name!=Some(table){continue;}
                let map=write.get("row").or_else(||write.get("patch")).and_then(|e|fields(e,bindings,0));
                let Some(map)=map else{*found=true;*coupled=true;continue;};
                if let Some(expression)=map.get(column){
                    *found=true;
                    if let Some(name)=expression.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)&&args.contains_key(name){
                        let occurrence=serde_json::to_string(&Expr::Variable(name.into())).unwrap();
                        if program.matches(&occurrence).count()!=1||guards.contains(name){*coupled=true;}
                    }else{*coupled=true;}
                }else if tag=="Upsert"||defaulted&&matches!(tag,"Insert"|"InsertIfAbsent"){*found=true;*coupled=true;}
            }
            for child in object.values(){writes(child,table,column,args,program,bindings,guards,defaulted,found,coupled);}
        }else if let Some(xs)=value.as_array(){for child in xs{writes(child,table,column,args,program,bindings,guards,defaulted,found,coupled);}}
    }
    let(mut found,mut unsafe_writer)=(false,false);
    for p in &bound.programs{let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();let text=serde_json::to_string(&p.program).unwrap();let mut bindings=BTreeMap::new();definitions(&json,&mut bindings);let guards=p.program.args.keys().filter(|arg|guarded(&json,arg,&inv.table,&p.program.args,&bindings)||related_write(&json,arg,&inv.table,column,&p.program.args,&bindings)).cloned().collect();writes(&json,&inv.table,column,&p.program.args,&text,&bindings,&guards,table.defaults.contains_key(column),&mut found,&mut unsafe_writer);}
    found&&unsafe_writer
}
fn run_pair_variant(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,presence:bool,out:&mut Campaign){
    if discharged(bound,inv){return;}
    if coupled(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: alternate value may break authored creator/guard coupling; only actual typed payloads and committed histories count",inv.target_label()));return;}
    if !reserve(out,1){return;}
    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
    let kind=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns[column];
    let mut other=match sample_rows(bound,rows,inv,row,presence){
        Some(other)=>other,
        None if kind.contains_asset()&&fixture.is_some()=>{
            let mut other=rows.clone();
            let Some(value)=sample(kind.required(),4)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media type unsupported",inv.table,column));return;};
            if let Some(Value::Row(fields))=other.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())){fields.insert(column.into(),value);}other

pub const RING_SIZE: usize = 256;
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, serde::Serialize)]
pub struct Change {
    pub preserved: Vec<String>,
    pub epoch: u64,
    pub incarnation: String,
    pub tables: Vec<String>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Snapshot {
    pub preserved: VecDeque<(u64, Vec<String>)>,
    pub identity: String,
    pub generation: u64,
    pub version: Version,
    pub ring: VecDeque<(u64, Vec<String>)>,
    pub tables: Vec<String>,
}
impl Snapshot {
    pub fn preserved_since(&self, epoch: u64, incarnation: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        if incarnation != self.version.incarnation || epoch >= self.version.epoch { return vec![]; }
        let mut result: Option<Vec<String>> = None;
        let mut previous = epoch;
        for (at, tables) in self.preserved.iter().filter(|(at,_)| *at > epoch) {
            if *at != previous + 1 { return vec![]; }
            if let Some(result) = &mut result { result.retain(|t| tables.contains(t)); } else { result = Some(tables.clone()); }
            previous = *at;
        }
        if previous != self.version.epoch { return vec![]; }
        result.unwrap_or_default()
    }

    pub fn changed(
        &self,
        epoch: u64,
        incarnation: Option<&str>,
        interest: Option<&BTreeSet<String>>,
    ) -> Vec<String> {
        let allowed = |name: &String| interest.is_none_or(|set| set.contains(name));
        if incarnation.is_some_and(|id| id != self.version.incarnation)
            || epoch > self.version.epoch
            || self
                .ring
                .front()
                .is_some_and(|(first, _)| epoch.saturating_add(1) < *first)
        {
            return match interest {
                Some(tables) => tables.iter().cloned().collect(),
                None => self.tables.clone(),
            };
        }
        self.ring
            .iter()
            .filter(|(at, _)| *at > epoch)
            .flat_map(|(_, tables)| tables.iter())
            .filter(|t| allowed(t))
            .cloned()
            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
            .into_iter()
            .collect()
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Limits {
    pub principal_rows: u64,
    pub principal_bytes: u64,
    pub rows: u64,
    pub bytes: u64,
}
impl Default for Limits {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            principal_rows: 1000,
            principal_bytes: 4 * 1024 * 1024,
            rows: 100_000,
            bytes: 512 * 1024 * 1024,
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
struct Count {
    rows: u64,
    bytes: u64,
}
impl Count {
    fn add(&mut self, bytes: u64) {
        self.rows += 1;
        self.bytes = self.bytes.saturating_add(bytes);
    }
    fn remove(&mut self, bytes: u64) {
        self.rows -= 1;
        self.bytes -= bytes;
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
struct Accounting {
    owners: BTreeMap<(String, String), (String, u64)>,
    principals: BTreeMap<String, Count>,
    total: Count,
}
struct Plan {
    changes: Vec<((String, String), Option<(String, u64)>)>,
    principals: BTreeMap<String, Count>,
    total: Count,
}
impl Accounting {
    fn remove(&mut self, key: &(String, String)) {
        if let Some((principal, bytes)) = self.owners.remove(key) {
            self.total.remove(bytes);
            let count = self.principals.get_mut(&principal).unwrap();
            count.remove(bytes);
            if count.rows == 0 {
                self.principals.remove(&principal);
            }
        }
    }
    fn plan(
        &self,
        layer: &Layered<'_>,
        schema: &Schema,
        viewer: &str,
        limits: Limits,
    ) -> Result<Plan, RuntimeError> {
        let mut plan = Plan {
            changes: Vec::new(),
            principals: BTreeMap::new(),
            total: self.total,
        };
        for key in layer.changed() {
            if let Some((owner, bytes)) = self.owners.get(&key) {
                plan.total.remove(*bytes);
                plan.principals
                    .entry(owner.clone())
                    .or_insert(self.principals[owner])
                    .remove(*bytes);
            }
            let owner = if layer.eph.get(&key.0, &key.1)?.is_some() {
                let size = declared_size(&schema.tables[&key.0]);
                plan.total.add(size);
                plan.principals
                    .entry(viewer.into())
                    .or_insert_with(|| self.principals.get(viewer).copied().unwrap_or_default())
                    .add(size);
                Some((viewer.into(), size))
            } else {
                None
            };
            plan.changes.push((key, owner));
        }
        if plan.total.rows > limits.rows
            || plan.total.bytes > limits.bytes
            || plan
                .principals
                .values()
                .any(|c| c.rows > limits.principal_rows || c.bytes > limits.principal_bytes)
        {
            return Err(full());
        }
        Ok(plan)
    }
    fn apply(&mut self, plan: Plan) {
        for (key, owner) in plan.changes {
            match owner {
                Some(owner) => {
                    self.owners.insert(key, owner);
                }
                None => {
                    self.owners.remove(&key);
                }
            }
        }
        for (owner, count) in plan.principals {
            if count.rows == 0 {
                self.principals.remove(&owner);
            } else {
                self.principals.insert(owner, count);
            }
        }
        self.total = plan.total;
    }
}
/// Prices the declared image plus every bounded index key; no payload is consulted.
pub fn declared_size(table: &Table) -> u64 {
    let image = super::write::target::row_copy_ceiling(table);
    table.indexes.values().fold(image, |total, index| {
        let key = index
            .columns()
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|c| table.columns.get(c))
            .fold(144u64, |n, t| {
                n.saturating_add(
                    16 + super::write::target::column_copy_ceiling(t).saturating_mul(2),
                )
            });
        total.saturating_add(64 + key.min(crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as u64))
    })
