fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if queries.is_empty(){return;}
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            if out.exhausted(){return;}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                        if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
    })
}
fn full() -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural {
        code: "E_EPHEMERAL_FULL",
        message: "ephemeral capacity is full; wait for expiry or delete your rows and retry".into(),
    }
}
fn incarnation() -> String {
    crate::ids::mint(crate::now_ms(), &mut crate::ids::Mint::default())
        .expect("current time is an id instant")
}

pub struct Hub {
    pub(super) state: Mutex<HubState>,
    limits: Limits,
}
pub struct HubState {
    pub(super) rows: MemoryRows,
    identity: String,
    generation: u64,
    tables: BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    accounting: Accounting,
    version: Version,
    ring: VecDeque<(u64, Vec<String>)>,
    preserved: VecDeque<(u64, Vec<String>)>,
    publisher: Option<Arc<dyn Fn(Snapshot) + Send + Sync>>,
}
impl HubState {
    fn new(identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema) -> Self {
        Self {
            rows: MemoryRows::new(),
            identity,
            generation,
            tables: schema
                .tables
                .iter()
                .filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_some())
                .map(|(n, t)| (n.clone(), t.clone()))
                .collect(),
            accounting: Accounting::default(),
            version: Version {
                epoch: 0,
                incarnation: incarnation(),
            },
            ring: VecDeque::new(),
            preserved: VecDeque::new(),
            publisher: None,
        }
    }
    pub fn version(&self) -> Version {
        self.version.clone()
    }
    pub fn snapshot(&self) -> Snapshot {
        Snapshot {
            identity: self.identity.clone(),
            generation: self.generation,
            version: self.version.clone(),
            ring: self.ring.clone(),
            preserved: self.preserved.clone(),
            tables: self.tables.keys().cloned().collect(),
        }
    }
    fn publish(&self) {
        if let Some(publish) = &self.publisher { publish(self.snapshot()); }
    }
    fn rebind(&mut self, identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema) {
        let publisher = self.publisher.take();
        *self = Self::new(identity, generation, schema);
        self.publisher = publisher;
        self.publish();
    }
    // Snapshot identity has already been established by the deferred transaction.
    pub fn bind(
        &mut self,
        identity: &str,
        generation: u64,
        schema: &Schema,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if identity != self.identity || generation < self.generation {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation(
                "ephemeral hub serves a newer store or generation; retry with a fresh reader"
                    .into(),
            ));
        }
        if generation > self.generation {
            self.rebind(identity.into(), generation, schema);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn sweep(&mut self, now: i64) -> Result<usize, RuntimeError> {
        let mut removed = 0;
        for (name, table) in &self.tables {
            loop {
                let rows = self.rows.scan(
                    name,
                    "$expiry",
                    &[],
                    &Bounds {
                        lte: Some(Value::Int(now)),
                        ..Bounds::default()
                    },
                    Dir::Asc,
                    256,
                )?;
                if rows.is_empty() {
                    break;
                }
                for (row, _) in rows {
                    let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
                    self.rows.delete(name, table, &row)?;
                    self.accounting.remove(&(name.clone(), id));
                    removed += 1;
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(removed)
    }
}
impl Hub {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — campaign copies preserve deadlines, quotas and epochs without retaining the live publisher.
    pub(super) fn fork_fixture(&self,identity:&str,generation:u64)->Result<Self,RuntimeError>{
        let state=self.lock()?;
        if state.identity!=identity||state.generation!=generation{return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs a hub bound to the selected store generation".into()));}
        Ok(Self{limits:self.limits,state:Mutex::new(HubState{rows:state.rows.fork_fixture(),identity:state.identity.clone(),generation:state.generation,tables:state.tables.clone(),accounting:state.accounting.clone(),version:state.version.clone(),ring:state.ring.clone(),preserved:state.preserved.clone(),publisher:None})})
    }
    pub fn new(identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema) -> Self {
        Self::with_limits(identity, generation, schema, Limits::default())
    }
    pub fn with_limits(identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema, limits: Limits) -> Self {
        Self {
            state: Mutex::new(HubState::new(identity, generation, schema)),
            limits,
        }
    }
    pub fn lock(&self) -> Result<MutexGuard<'_, HubState>, RuntimeError> {
        self.state
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| RuntimeError::Store("ephemeral hub lock poisoned".into()))
    }
    pub fn snapshot(&self) -> Result<Snapshot, RuntimeError> {
        Ok(self.lock()?.snapshot())
    }
    /// Install before serving. Called under the hub lock after each commit or
    /// rebind; the callback may take the wake mutex, never the hub or engine.
    // @ref LLP 3000.017#3.3 — publish before releasing the hub.
    pub fn set_publisher(&self, publisher: impl Fn(Snapshot) + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let mut hub = self.lock()?;
        hub.publisher = Some(Arc::new(publisher));
        hub.publish();
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn clear_and_rebind(
        &self,
        identity: String,
        generation: u64,
        schema: &Schema,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.lock()?.rebind(identity, generation, schema);
        Ok(())
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — never hold the hub across SQLite activation.
    // Readers take SQLite before the hub. Even BEGIN IMMEDIATE only reserves
    // the DELETE-mode writer: commit/spill still needs readers to release it.
    // Adoption checks poison before activation, then releases the hub. A panic
    // racing that check cannot turn a committed adoption into a refusal: rebind
    // replaces all ephemeral state, so discard poisoned state and clear poison.
    pub(super) fn rebind_committed(&self, identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema) {
        let mut hub = self.state.lock().unwrap_or_else(std::sync::PoisonError::into_inner);
        hub.rebind(identity, generation, schema);
        self.state.clear_poison();
    }
    pub fn sweep(&self, now: i64) -> Result<usize, RuntimeError> {
        self.lock()?.sweep(now)
    }
}

pub struct Outcome {
    pub result: Value,
    pub ephemeral: Change,
    pub metered: Metered,
    pub seq: u64,
}
impl Engine {
    pub fn ephemeral_hub(&self) -> Arc<Hub> {
        self.ephemeral.clone()
//! A campaign fork retains activated semantics without running adoption again.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — isolation includes entropy, assets and ephemeral ownership.
    pub fn fork_fixture(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        if self.presence.is_some(){return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated external presence snapshot".into()));}
        let store=self.store.fork_memory()?;
        let identity=store.meta("store_id")?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture store has no identity".into()))?;
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule:timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral:std::sync::Arc::new(if self.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.ephemeral.is_some()){self.ephemeral.fork_fixture(&identity,self.generation)?}else{ephemeral::Hub::new(identity,self.generation,&self.schema)}),
            store,schema:self.schema.clone(),programs:self.programs.clone(),ceilings:self.ceilings,
            entropy:crate::ids::seed(),minter:crate::ids::Mint::default(),generation:self.generation,
            expiry_after:self.expiry_after.clone(),dev_effects:self.dev_effects,
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native:self.native.clone(),
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native_logs:native::Logs::default(),presence:None,
        })
    }
}

impl Engine {
    /// Judge independent fixture intent with the engine's authorized rule hops.
    pub fn fixture_policy(&mut self,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,next:&Value,now:i64)->Result<rules::Verdict,RuntimeError>{
        let tx=self.store.read_transaction()?;
        let context=if viewer==crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL{Context::system()}else{Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new())};
        let mut runner=Runner::new(&tx,&self.schema,context.at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(),Cut::Abort),0);
        let bytes=runner.rule_scratch(rule);
        runner.authorized_scratch(bytes,&[],|runner,allowance|{
            let mut probe=read::RuleProbe{runner,allowance};
            rules::evaluate_verdict(rule,viewer,old,next,false,&mut probe)
        })
    }
}

//! Narrow stable leading-require reduction for operation-specific authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown write sets never establish stability.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Stmt,ProgramDecl,ProgramKind,InvariantDecl,RuleDecl};
use super::super::catalog::TableInfo;
pub(super) fn resolve<'a>(catalog:&'a Catalog,decl:&InvariantDecl)->Option<(String,&'a ProgramDecl)>{
    if matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"always"|"write"){return None;}
    let matches=catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|{let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();d.programs.iter().map(move|p|(super::super::operation_name(&module,&p.name),p))})
        .filter(|(name,p)|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(name==&decl.target||p.name==decl.target)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if matches.len()==1 {matches.into_iter().next()}else{None}
}
pub(super) fn safe(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {match rule{
    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(safe),
    RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>[a,b].iter().all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}
pub(super) fn requirements(catalog:&Catalog,info:&TableInfo,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){let Stmt::Require{condition,site,..}=s else{break};
        let decl=RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:condition.clone(),site:site.clone()};
        if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&decl)&&safe(&rule){out.push(rule);}
    }out
}
pub(super) fn prove(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<bool,String>{
    let p=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|Some(&p.program.name)==inv.operation.as_ref()).ok_or("operation did not bind")?;
    if !safe(inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("unsupported policy")?){return Err("operation policy needs unsupported target-image, negative-probe or live-reference bindings".into());}
    let writes=snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program);
    if !writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table){return Err("operation has no direct write to the named table; implicit effects require a reduction".into());}
    let dependencies=inv.requirements.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|dependencies.contains(t)) {return Err("require dependency can be changed by this operation, including its target write".into());}
    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.maintained.is_empty()||!t.sequences.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())) || !bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){return Err("implicit maintain/sequence/referential writes require dependency stability reduction".into());}
    // Source helpers/control flow may hide write sets or viewer changes.
    let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool{match v{serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"For"|"If"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"|"Emit"))||m.values().any(opaque),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false}}
    if opaque(&json){return Err("operation control flow or opaque effects are outside stable leading-require reduction".into());}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(inv.requirements.clone());
    // Table-rule gates can only further restrict a dominating stable require;
    // using the require alone overapproximates effective owner-field gates.
    kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())
}

impl Invariant {
    pub fn target_label(&self)->String {match &self.column{Some(c)=>format!("{}.{} {}",self.table,c,self.target),None=>format!("{}.{}",self.table,self.target)}}
}
pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Vec<Invariant> {
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for decl in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.invariants) {
        let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&decl.table) else {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET",format!("invariant names unknown table {}",decl.table),"declare the table or correct its name").guide("security")); continue;
        };
        let named_operation=operation::resolve(catalog,decl);
        if (!matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && named_operation.is_none())
            || decl.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"&&!info.columns.contains_key(c)) {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET","invariant needs a declared column or table effect","use invariant table.read only <policy>, table.column write only <policy>, or table always <predicate>").guide("security")); continue;
        }
        let effect=match decl.target.as_str(){"insert"=>ast::Effect::Insert,"update"|"write"=>ast::Effect::Update,"delete"=>ast::Effect::Delete,_=>ast::Effect::Read};
        // Independent intent may use authorized reference operands even when a
        // runtime read gate must use captured authority. It never installs a gate.
        let effect=if effect==ast::Effect::Read{ast::Effect::Insert}else{effect};
        let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect,bindings:Vec::new(),value:decl.value.clone(),site:decl.site.clone()};
        let (policy,unsupported)=match super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule) {
            Ok(rule)=>(Some(rule),None),Err(error)=>match temporal::bind(catalog,info,&decl.value) {
                Some(rule) if decl.target=="read" => (Some(rule),None),
                _ => (None,Some(format!("policy outside rule proof fragment: {} ({})",error.message,error.code))),
            },
        };
        let requirements=named_operation.as_ref().map(|(_,p)| operation::requirements(catalog,info,p)).unwrap_or_default();
        out.push(Invariant{operation:named_operation.map(|(name,_)|name),requirements,syntax:Some(decl.value.clone()),table:decl.table.clone(),target:decl.target.clone(),column:decl.column.clone(),predicate:crate::print::expression(&decl.value),site:decl.site.clone(),policy,unsupported});
    }
    out
}

1425:// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — durable and ephemeral seats receive the same static clause for every private cause.
//! Isolated complete fixtures, including captured authority and asset ownership.
use super::*;
impl Store {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — fork committed state, never reinterpret stored rows as new seed input.
    pub fn fork_memory(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let mut fork = Self::memory()?;
        {
            let backup = rusqlite::backup::Backup::new(&self.conn, &mut fork.conn)?;
            backup.run_to_completion(256, std::time::Duration::ZERO, None)?;
        }
        fork.catalog = self.catalog.clone();
        fork.identity = self.identity.clone();
        fork.server = self.server;
        if self.assets_dir.exists() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fork.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)? {
                let entry = entry.map_err(io)?;
                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file() {
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset ledger contains a non-file".into()));
                }
                std::fs::copy(entry.path(), fork.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(fork)
    }
}

fn io(e:std::io::Error)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string())}

impl Store {
    /// Independent valid worlds share verified bytes, never source row state.
    pub fn empty_with_asset_ledger(&self)->Result<Self,RuntimeError>{
        let fresh=Self::memory()?;
        let ids=self.conn.prepare("SELECT id FROM _assets")?.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
        for id in ids{
            let mut asset=self.asset(&id)?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset disappeared".into()))?;
            asset.home=None;asset.released_at=None;
            fresh.record_asset(&asset)?;
        }
        if self.assets_dir.exists(){
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fresh.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)?{
                let entry=entry.map_err(io)?;
                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file(){return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset is not a regular file".into()));}
                std::fs::copy(entry.path(),fresh.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(fresh)
    }

fn activate(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    release: u64,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    revision: u64,
    bound: snapback4_lang::Bound,
    rollback: bool,
    restore_dormant: bool,
    allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    options: &Options,
    mut candidate: Option<&mut crate::release_files::Candidate>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let root = dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string());
    let digest = crate::release_files::digest(&root)?;
    if let Some(owner) = owner {
        let project = Project { root };
        let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
        crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
        crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
        let mut pointer = crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, previous, rollback)?;
        let activation = snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation {
            release,
            expected_previous: previous,
            expected_revision: Some(revision),
            restore_dormant,
            native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
            operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
        };
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared(
            store,
            bound.schema,
            bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
            &activation,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        pointer.finish(engine.generation())?;
        receipt(release,engine.generation(),&engine.store().adoption_report,options);

    } else {
        let result = crate::release_control::request_observed(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Activate {
                release,
                expected_release: previous,
                expected_revision: revision,
                digest,
                rollback,
                restore_dormant,
                allow_feed_adoption,
            },
            |pending| { if let Some(candidate)=candidate.as_deref_mut() {candidate.pending=pending;} },
        )?;
        if options.json {println!("{}",result);} else {
            println!("release {release} is current as generation {}; {}; running serve adopted the release",result["generation"],result["adoption"].as_str().unwrap_or("adopted"));
            for fill in result["backfills"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().filter_map(|f|f.as_str()) {println!("  backfill: {fill}");}
            if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {println!("{}",result["details"].as_str().unwrap_or(""));}
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn report(verb: &str, result: Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
    match result {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}

//! Paired logical observations, replayed independently in memory.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — two valid worlds and repeatable observations are required.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Observations {
    pub present: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent_rows: BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,
}

pub(super) fn observe(engine:&mut Engine,p:&Program,viewer:&str,args:BTreeMap<String,Value>)->Result<serde_json::Value,String>{
    if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&engine.schema().operations.get(&p.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral){return Err("ephemeral mutation observer is outside the durable campaign".into());}
    let context=Context::new(viewer,args).at(NOW);
    if p.kind==ProgramKind::Query {
        match engine.query(&p.name,context) {
            Ok(q) if q.unavailable || !q.complete => Err("incomplete/metered query is outside logical payload comparison".into()),
            Ok(q)=>Ok(serde_json::json!({"data":q.data})),
            Err(e) if e.rule.is_some() && matches!(e.family.as_str(),"rule"|"constraint") => Ok(serde_json::json!({"refusal":{"code":e.code,"message":e.message}})),
            Err(e)=>Err(format!("query error is not an admitted observation: {e}")),
        }
    }else{
        match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,context).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.state {
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{result,..}=>Ok(serde_json::json!({"sent":result})),
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{why,current}=>{
                // E_RULE can also mask authorization-budget exhaustion. Only
                // an authored require/constraint carries this rule marker;
                // bound/storage/schema/ambiguous engine refusals cannot be a
                // logical counterexample in our resource-excluding contract.
                if why.rule.is_none()||!matches!(why.family.as_str(),"rule"|"constraint") {
                    return Err(format!("resource/engine or ambiguous mutation refusal {} is outside paired logical comparison",why.code));
                }
                Ok(serde_json::json!({"refusal":{"code":why.code,"message":why.message},"current":current}))
            },
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c=="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
        if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation needs complete bound policy dependencies; unsupported policy AST is not dependency-free",inv.table));continue;}
        if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
        // Closed transitive observer set: include maintained targets, inherited
        // readers and every table whose rule probes an affected population.
        // An operation mentioning none of them has no logical input changed
        // by this pair. This prunes experiments, never proves an obligation.
        let mut observers=BTreeSet::from([inv.table.clone()]);
        loop {
            let before=observers.len();
            for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if observers.contains(&m.source){
                observers.insert(m.target.clone());
                if let Some(owner)=&m.private{observers.insert(owner.table.clone());}
            }}
            for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables {
                if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|observers.contains(&p.parent))
                    || [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|d|observers.contains(d))) {
                    observers.insert(name.clone());
                }
            }
            if observers.len()==before{break;}
        }
        // Removing an ordinary row must not manufacture an invalid reference
        // graph. More general paired deletions need a checked closure.
        if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))) {
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired absence needs a referentially valid world closure",inv.table));continue;
        }
        for variant in 0..8 {
            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
            let Ok(present)=snapshot(bound,inv,variant)else{continue;};
            if present.keys().any(|name|bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values().any(C::contains_asset)) {
                out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired fixture asset identities are not coupled",inv.table));continue;
            }
            let Some(row)=present.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first()).cloned()else{continue;};
            let prepared=(||->Result<Value,String>{
                let e=engine(bound,&present,program("$security.pair.image",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?;
//! Real upload ledgers for foreign-id and non-image placement challenges.
// @ref LLP 3000.038#3-upload — assets retain upload ownership and verified media type.
use super::*;
const PDF:&[u8]=b"%PDF-1.4\n%%EOF\n";
pub(super) fn file(e:&mut Engine,owner:&str,types:&[String])->Result<Value,String>{
    let mime=types.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("application/pdf");
    let (bytes,name)=match mime{"application/pdf"=>(PDF,"proof.pdf"),"text/plain"=>(b"verified text\n".as_slice(),"proof.txt"),"image/png"=>(PNG.as_slice(),"proof.png"),_=>return Err(format!("file ledger needs a verified {mime} fixture"))};
    e.store_file(owner,bytes,Some(mime),name,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn contains(value:&Value,id:&Value)->bool {
    if value==id{return true;}
    match value{Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Map(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Row(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    if initial.values().any(|xs|!xs.is_empty()){
        out.unsupported.insert("foreign-asset replay over populated fixtures needs full ledger transfer; empty-ledger challenges run separately".into());
    }
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let p=&bp.program;
        if ephemeral_operation(bound,p,out){continue;}
        for (arg,kind) in &p.args{
            if !matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}|ir::ArgType::File{..}){continue;}
            for mode in ["foreign upload","non-image photo"]{
                if mode=="non-image photo"&&!matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}){continue;}
                let mut e=match engine(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),program("$security.asset",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let sample=if mode=="non-image photo"{file(&mut e,VIEWER,&[])}else{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Image{..}=>e.store_asset(OTHER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
                    ir::ArgType::File{types,..}=>file(&mut e,OTHER,types),_=>unreachable!(),
                }};
                let sample=match sample{Ok(v)=>v,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let Some(mut args)=arguments(&p.args,0)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge needs remaining argument fixtures",p.name));continue;};
                args.insert(arg.clone(),sample.clone());
                out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                match e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(VIEWER,args).at(NOW)){
                    Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>out.refused+=1,
                    Ok(_)=>{if let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)&&rows.values().flatten().any(|row|contains(row,&sample)){
                        out.boundary_failures.push(format!("{} placed {mode} through argument {arg}",p.name));
                    }},
                    Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge engine error {error}",p.name));},
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
pub(super) fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn fixture_cases(p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    let args=&p.args;
    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
            }
        }}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    }
    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match k{
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..}=>['z','y'].into_iter().map(|c|Value::String(c.to_string().repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize))).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,k) in args{
        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
            let valid=match (k,literal){
                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
            };
            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
        }
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
        }}
        cases=next;
    }cases
}
pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});true
}
fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)));
    match ty{
        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key,column,physical),
        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key,column,physical))),
        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key,column,physical))),
        _=>false,
    }
}

    /// native host one chunk per commit (LLP 3000.005). The body is empty.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_false")]
    pub native: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ProgramKind {
    Query,
    Mutation,
    #[serde(rename = "job")]
    Job,
    #[serde(rename = "action")]
    Action,
}

impl ProgramKind {
    pub fn word(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Query => "query",
            Self::Mutation => "mutation",
            Self::Job => "job",
            Self::Action => "action",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ArgType {
    Cursor,
    Id,
    Principal,
    Ref { table: String },
    Text {
        max: u32,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_zero")]
        min: u32,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none", deserialize_with = "deserialize_text_format")]
        format: Option<crate::schema::TextFormat>,
    },
    Bytes { max: u32 },
    Image { max: u32 },
    Video { max: u32 },
    File {
        max: u32,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
        types: Vec<String>,
    },
    Int,
    // @ref LLP 3000.040#13-representation — a new tag: an older device cannot decode the program, so it never skips the bound.
    IntRange { #[serde(default = "crate::schema::int_floor", skip_serializing_if = "crate::schema::is_int_floor")] min: i64, #[serde(default = "crate::schema::int_ceiling", skip_serializing_if = "crate::schema::is_int_ceiling")] max: i64 },
    Bool,
    Time,
    Json { max_bytes: u32 },
    Optional(Box<ArgType>),
    Scalar,
    List {
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        of: Option<Box<ArgType>>,
        max: u32,
    },
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — preserve List bytes at min zero; fail closed on older readers.
    ListRange {
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        of: Option<Box<ArgType>>,
        min: u32,
        max: u32,
    },
    Enum { variants: Vec<String> },
    Decimal { scale: u8 },
    Money { currency: String, scale: u8 },
}

impl ArgType {
    pub fn bounds_valid(&self) -> bool {
        match self {
            Self::Text { min, max, .. } => min <= max,
            Self::IntRange { min, max } => crate::schema::int_range_valid(*min, *max),
            Self::ListRange { of, min, max } => min <= max && of.as_ref().is_none_or(|of| of.bounds_valid()),
            Self::Optional(inner) | Self::List { of: Some(inner), .. } => inner.bounds_valid(),
            _ => true,
        }
    }

    pub fn is_optional(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Optional(_))
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Stmt {
    Const { name: String, value: Expr },
    Let { name: String, value: Expr },
    Assign { name: String, value: Expr },
    Push { target: String, value: Expr },
    If { condition: Expr, then_body: Vec<Stmt>, else_body: Vec<Stmt> },
    For { binding: String, domain: LoopDomain, body: Vec<Stmt> },
    Require {
        condition: Expr,
        code: String,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        site: Option<String>,
        /// The author's text for the refusal (`require … else CODE 'message'`); the code stays the contract.
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        message: Option<String>,
    },
    /// A false predicate rolls the transaction back and re-reads `current`.
    Check { condition: Expr, current: Read },
    /// A unique clash yields absence, never the conflicting row.
    InsertIfAbsent { table: String, row: Expr, result: String },
    Insert { table: String, row: Expr },
    Update { table: String, key: Expr, patch: Expr },
    Upsert { table: String, key: Expr, row: Expr },
    /// Target lookup and the old image stay inside the engine. Only a read-filtered result is bound.
    WriteTarget { target: Read, patch: Expr, upsert: bool, condition: Option<WriteCondition>, result: Option<String> },
    Delete { table: String, key: Expr },
    /// Alternate-key delete resolves inside the engine before charging hidden expiry work.
    DeleteTarget { target: Read },
    Emit { effect: String, payload: Expr, #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] once: Option<Expr> },
    Return(Expr),
    Break,
    Continue,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WriteCondition {
    pub old: String,
    pub setup: Vec<Stmt>,
    pub value: Expr,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum LoopDomain {
    Page(Scan),
    LiteralRange { start: i64, end_exclusive: i64 },
    ExpressionRange { start: Expr, end_exclusive: Expr },
    ArgList { name: String, max: u32 },
    DerivedArray { value: Expr, max: u32, bound_source: String },
        for (name,kind) in &p.args {if let Some(value)=args.get(name){match kind {
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>{if let Value::Id(actor)=value{recipients.insert(actor.clone());}},
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rosters.contains(table)=>{
                if let Some(row)=initial.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(value)){
                    for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(kind.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(actor))=row.field(column){recipients.insert(actor.clone());}}
                }
            },_=>{}
        }}}
        let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
        let successors=candidates.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(j,(next,actor,_))|(next.name!=p.name&&recipients.contains(actor)&&seen.insert((next.name.clone(),actor.clone()))).then_some((i,j))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if !successors.is_empty(){role_groups.push(successors);}
    }
    let mut role_pairs=Vec::new();
    'pairs:for n in 0..role_groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &role_groups{if let Some(pair)=group.get(n){role_pairs.push(*pair);if role_pairs.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'pairs;}}}}
    // Breadth first, then targeted and diagonal pairs: no first step owns the cap.
    let history_limit=if out.history_limit==0{LIMIT}else{out.history_limit.min(LIMIT)};
    let singles=candidates.len().min(history_limit);
    let mut setups=Vec::new();let mut role_changes=Vec::new();
    let mut setup_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut role_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut pair_plan=Vec::new();
    for n in 0..singles+history_limit {
        if !out.reserve(1){break;}
        if n==singles&&!candidates.is_empty(){
            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
            'setup:for &second in &role_changes{for &first in &setups{if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'setup;}}}}
            for &(first,second) in &role_pairs{if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT{break;}if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));}}
            let mut k=0;
            while pair_plan.len()<LIMIT{pair_plan.push((k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()));k+=1;}
        }
        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let (first,second)=pair_plan[n-singles];vec![first,second]};
        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
        for i in indices{
            if !out.reserve(1){break;}
            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){if !out.reserve(1){break;}match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
                Ok(result)=>{
                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
                    if sent&&n<singles{
                        let signature=serde_json::to_string(&(&p.name,viewer,&candidates[i].2)).unwrap();
                        let new_media=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|inv.target=="read"&&initial.get(&inv.table).is_none_or(Vec::is_empty)).any(|inv|{
                            let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|table.columns.iter().any(|(column,kind)|kind.contains_asset()&&row.field(column).is_ok_and(|v|*v!=Value::Null&&!matches!(v,Value::Array(xs) if xs.is_empty()))))
                        });
                        if new_media&&setup_seen.insert(signature.clone()){setups.push(i);}
                        let changed_role=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,_)|{
                            let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
                            !columns.is_empty()&&before.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|columns.iter().any(|c|old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))
                        });
                        if changed_role&&role_seen.insert(signature){role_changes.push(i);}
                    }
                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
            }
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
}

//! Role authority is compared by effective gates, never by names like 'admin'.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — advisory grant-edge analysis, not a security proof or implicit exemption.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn probe_pairs(bound:&Bound,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[RuleTerm],equalities:&[(String,RuleTerm)])->Vec<(String,RuleTerm)>{
    let mut pairs=equalities.to_vec();
    if let Some(idx)=bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t|t.index(index)){for (component,term) in idx.components.iter().zip(key){if let Some(column)=component.column(){pairs.push((column.into(),term.clone()));}}}
    pairs
}
pub(super) fn global_principal(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    let t=bound.schema.table(table)?;
    t.indexes.values().find_map(|index|{
        if !index.unique||index.components.len()!=1{return None;}
        let column=index.components[0].column()?;
        matches!(t.columns.get(column),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).then(||column.to_string())
    })
}
pub(super) fn membership_probe(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{
    let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=rule else{return false;};
    if table!=roster{return false;}
    let Some(principal)=global_principal(bound,table)else{return false;};
    let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
    pairs.len()==1&&pairs[0].0==principal&&pairs[0].1==RuleTerm::Viewer
}
// @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — ownership is required for every supplied owned field;
// only an exclusively owned patch bypasses an unnamed row update gate.
fn write_admission(table:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,op:ast::WriteOp,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->RuleExpr{
    let touched=fields.iter().filter(|f|!f.spread&&f.name!="id").map(|f|f.name.as_str()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let owned_only=!touched.is_empty()&&!fields.iter().any(|f|f.spread)&&touched.iter().all(|f|table.written_by.contains_key(*f));
    let gate=match op{
        ast::WriteOp::Insert=>table.rules.insert.clone(),
        ast::WriteOp::Update if owned_only&&!table.update_transition=>RuleExpr::Allow,
        ast::WriteOp::Update=>table.update_rule_judged().into_owned(),
        _=>RuleExpr::Deny,
    };
    let mut owners=table.written_by.iter().filter(|(field,authority)|touched.contains(field.as_str())||op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&!authority.audience&&table.rules.update.needs_field_value(field,&table.columns[*field])).map(|(_,authority)|RuleExpr::Eq(if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{RuleTerm::Field(authority.owner.clone())}else{RuleTerm::OldField(authority.owner.clone())},RuleTerm::Viewer)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    owners.push(RuleExpr::Or(gate.disjuncts().iter().filter(|r|r.service_grant().is_none()).map(|r|(*r).clone()).collect()));
    RuleExpr::And(owners)
}
pub(super) fn membership_grant_risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,operation:&str,requirements:&[RuleExpr],op:ast::WriteOp,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->bool{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Insert,fields)||membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Update,fields);}
    fn honors(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{match rule{
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster)),
        r=>membership_probe(bound,r,roster),
    }}
    if global_principal(bound,roster).is_none()||!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster))){return false;}
    let table=&bound.schema.tables[roster];
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(write_admission(table,op,fields)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&global_principal(bound,roster).is_some_and(|column|kernel::implies(&admission,&RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.clone()),RuleTerm::Field(column)))==Ok(true)){return false;}
    let ordinary=substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster","$membership",None,None);
    bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.is_none()&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).any(|p|super::operation::safe(p)&&kernel::implies(&ordinary,p)==Ok(false))
}
fn substitute(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,role:Option<&str>,target:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
    let known=|t:&RuleTerm|match t{RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c) if c==column=>target.map(|s|snapback4_core::Value::String(s.into())),RuleTerm::Literal(v)=>Some(v.clone()),_=>None};
    match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,index,equalities,..} if table==roster&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
            let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
            let requested=pairs.iter().find(|(c,_)|c==column).and_then(|(_,t)|if let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{Some(s.as_str())}else{None});
            if requested.is_none(){rule.clone()}else if requested==role{
                if pairs.iter().all(|(c,t)|c==column||matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{rule.clone()}
            }else{RuleExpr::Deny}
        },
        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)),RuleTerm::Viewer) if seat.starts_with("service:")=>RuleExpr::Deny,
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(known(a),known(b)){if (a==b)==matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Eq(..)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}}else{rule.clone()},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Not(r)=>RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target))),
        _=>rule.clone(),
    }
}
pub(super) fn risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str,operation:&str,targets:&[String],op:ast::WriteOp,requirements:&[RuleExpr],dynamic:bool,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->Option<String>{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Insert,requirements,dynamic,fields).or_else(||risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Update,requirements,dynamic,fields));}
    let table=bound.schema.table(roster)?;
    if requirements.iter().any(|r|!r.disjuncts().is_empty()&&r.disjuncts().iter().all(|b|b.service_grant().is_some())){return None;}
    let roles=role_values(bound,roster,column);
    let intents=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c==column)&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    fn role_guards(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut Vec<RuleExpr>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|role_columns(bound,table).contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))))=>out.push(rule.clone()),
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{role_guards(bound,r,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(write_admission(table,op,fields)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());

pub const MAX_RECURSE_DEPTH: u32 = 10_000;

//! The same fixture loader serves query testing and residency at either CLI entry point.
use super::Options;
use crate::project::Project;
use snapback4_core::{Engine, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;

pub(super) fn engine(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    engine_at(project, bound, options, snapback4_core::now_ms())
}

pub(super) fn engine_at(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture") else {
        if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
        }
        return project.open_engine(bound).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open the store: {error}"));
    };
    let label=options.flags.get("fixture-input-label").unwrap_or(fixture);
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut schema = bound.schema.clone();
    // Residency defers ordinary maintains until after bulk loading and builds
    // them once; column derives stay installed and maintain as rows insert.
    let residency = options.flags.contains_key("residency");
    if residency { schema.maintains.retain(|m| m.private.is_some()); }
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut engine = Engine::new_at(store, schema, bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(fixture).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {label}: {error}"))? {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {label}: {error}"))?;
        names.push(entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string());
    }
    names.sort();
    // One batch for the whole directory: the engine orders parents before
    // children within a batch, so file order (and read_dir order) cannot make
    // a read-through child or a reference miss its parent.
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    let mut unknown = Vec::new();
    for name in names {
        let Some(table) = name.strip_suffix(".jsonl") else { continue };
        if name == super::test_cases::FILE { continue; }
        let path = std::path::Path::new(fixture).join(&name);
        let shown_path=std::path::Path::new(label).join(&name);
        let Some(definition) = bound.schema.tables.get(table) else {
            if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") {
                eprintln!("test: skipped {} — saved source declares no table {table}; pass --fixture <dir> to select contemporary fixtures explicitly", shown_path.display());
                continue;
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — the starter's own unmodified sample is skipped loudly; a project's file for a missing table still refuses.
            if !options.flags.contains_key("source") && super::init::starter_fixture(&name).is_some_and(|starter| std::fs::read_to_string(&path).is_ok_and(|text| text == starter)) {
                eprintln!("test: WARNING: skipped {} — the todo starter's unmodified sample rows for a `{table}` table your snapback/*.q does not declare; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's other leftovers", shown_path.display());
                continue;
            }
            unknown.push(name);
            continue;
        };
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture file {}: {error}", shown_path.display()))?;
        for (line_number, line) in text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty()) {
            let at = format!("{name}:{}", line_number + 1);
            let mut json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}"))?;
            // A residency fixture is often an exported store: engine-maintained
            // columns are dropped and rebuilt from the source rows. `test`
            // refuses them as an insert and `/seed` do (E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN).
            if residency && let Some(fields) = json.as_object_mut() { fields.retain(|field, _| !definition.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
            relative_times(definition, &mut json, now).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
            if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &json) { return Err(format!("{at}: {why}")); }
            let row = wire::row_from_json(definition, &json).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}\n  hint: a fixture row is one JSON object per line with the table's fields and an \"id\"; see snapback4 guide testing"))?;
            rows.push((table.to_owned(), row));
        }
    }
    // A misspelled file name would otherwise load nothing and still pass.
    if !unknown.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<&str> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        // 0.4.17's init wrote the todo starter's rows beside a project's own schema.
        let starter = unknown.iter().any(|name| name == "todos.jsonl")
            && std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(fixture).join("todos.jsonl")).is_ok_and(|text| text.contains("\"id\":\"todo-milk\""));
        let starter = if starter { format!("\n  hint: todos.jsonl is the todo starter's sample that `snapback4 init` wrote; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's {} while they are unmodified (or delete them, and the starter's list/add/toggle/remove lines, yourself)", super::test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
        return Err(format!("{label}: {} name{} no table in snapback/*.q; nothing was run{starter}\n  hint: a fixture file is <table>.jsonl for a declared table ({}), plus {}; see snapback4 guide testing",
            unknown.join(", "), if unknown.len() == 1 { "s" } else { "" }, tables.join(", "), super::test_cases::FILE));
    }
    // Fixture rows take `/seed`'s insert path (defaults, sequences, unique with the author's codes, caps, maintained views), not the trusted bulk import.
    let loaded = engine.seed_at(rows, now).map_err(|error| {
        let text = error.to_string();
        let hint = if text.contains("NOT_FOUND") { "\n  hint: every fixture file loads as one batch, parents first; NOT_FOUND means a referenced or read-through parent row is in no fixture file (check the id)" } else { "" };
        format!("{label}: {text}{hint}")
    })?;
    if residency {
        engine.adopt_at(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot build fixture maintains: {error}"))?;
    }
    if !options.json { println!("test: loaded {loaded} rows from {label} in {:.1} s", started.elapsed().as_secs_f64()); }
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#5-fixtures-and-dev-evidence — relative time leaves, seeds and operations share one captured clock.
/// Only typed time leaves interpret `now`: text and JSON retain their literals.
pub(crate) fn relative_times(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, row: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = row.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(name) { time_value(kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?; }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn time_value(kind: &snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType, value: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) if !value.is_null() => time_value(inner, value, now),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, serde_json::Value::Array(items)) => {
            for item in items { time_value(of, item, now)?; }
//! An isolated production host for signed-out challenges. No development doors.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#4-counterexamples-and-generated-tests — signed-out contexts must cross HTTP admission.
use std::{fs, io::{Read,Write}, net::{TcpListener,TcpStream,SocketAddr}, path::PathBuf,
    process::{Child,Command,Stdio}, time::{Duration,Instant,SystemTime,UNIX_EPOCH}};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;
#[path="http_image.rs"] mod image;

struct Scratch(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Scratch {fn drop(&mut self){let _=fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
struct Process(Child);
impl Drop for Process {fn drop(&mut self){let _=self.0.kill();let _=self.0.wait();}}
fn wait(child:&mut Process,budget:Duration)->Result<(),String>{
    let until=Instant::now()+budget;
    loop{match child.0.try_wait().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?{
        Some(status)=>return if status.success(){Ok(())}else{Err(format!("private serve preparation exited {status}"))},
        None if Instant::now()<until=>std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)),
        _=>return Err("private serve preparation exceeded 30 seconds".into()),
    }}
}
fn request(address:SocketAddr,path:&str,persona:bool)->Result<(u16,serde_json::Value),String>{
    exchange(address,"POST",path,if persona{"x-snapback-persona: security-forged\r\n"}else{""},"{\"id\":\"security-unauthenticated\",\"args\":{}}")
}
fn exchange(address:SocketAddr,method:&str,path:&str,header:&str,body:&str)->Result<(u16,serde_json::Value),String>{
    let mut stream=TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address,Duration::from_secs(2)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(3))).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(3))).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let wire=format!("{method} {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n{header}\r\n{body}",body.len());
    stream.write_all(wire.as_bytes()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let mut bytes=Vec::new();stream.take(1<<20).read_to_end(&mut bytes).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    if bytes.len()==1<<20{return Err("private HTTP response exceeded 1 MiB".into());}
    let boundary=bytes.windows(4).position(|b|b==b"\r\n\r\n").ok_or("malformed HTTP response")?;
    let headers=std::str::from_utf8(&bytes[..boundary]).map_err(|_|"invalid HTTP headers")?;
    let body=&bytes[boundary+4..];
    let status=headers.split_whitespace().nth(1).and_then(|s|s.parse::<u16>().ok()).ok_or("malformed HTTP status")?;
    let decoded=framed(headers,body)?;
    let json=serde_json::from_slice(&decoded).map_err(|e|format!("HTTP {status}: {e}"))?;
    Ok((status,json))
}
fn framed(headers:&str,body:&[u8])->Result<Vec<u8>,String>{
    let mut length=None;let mut transfer=false;
    let mut lines=headers.split("\r\n");
    let status=lines.next().ok_or("missing HTTP status line")?;
    if !(status.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 ")||status.starts_with("HTTP/1.0 "))||status.bytes().any(|b|b<32||b==127){return Err("malformed HTTP status line".into());}
    for line in lines{
        let (name,value)=line.split_once(':').ok_or("malformed HTTP header")?;
        if name.is_empty()||!name.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_alphanumeric()||b"!#$%&'*+-.^_`|~".contains(&b))||value.bytes().any(|b|b<32&&b!=b'\t'||b==127){return Err("malformed HTTP header field".into());}
        if name.eq_ignore_ascii_case("content-length"){
            let value=value.trim_matches([' ','\t']);
            if length.is_some()||value.is_empty()||!value.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_digit()){return Err("invalid or duplicate HTTP content length".into());}
            length=Some(value.parse::<usize>().map_err(|_|"invalid HTTP content length")?);
        }
        if name.eq_ignore_ascii_case("transfer-encoding"){
            if transfer||!value.trim_matches([' ','\t']).eq_ignore_ascii_case("chunked"){return Err("invalid or duplicate HTTP transfer encoding".into());}transfer=true;
        }
    }
    if transfer{
        if length.is_some(){return Err("conflicting HTTP body framing".into());}chunks(body)
    }else{
        if length!=Some(body.len()){return Err("HTTP body lacks complete declared content length".into());}
        Ok(body.to_vec())
    }
}
fn chunks(mut wire:&[u8])->Result<Vec<u8>,String>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    loop{
        let line=wire.windows(2).position(|b|b==b"\r\n").ok_or("incomplete HTTP chunk header")?;
        let size=std::str::from_utf8(&wire[..line]).map_err(|_|"invalid HTTP chunk header")?;
        if size.is_empty()||!size.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_hexdigit()){return Err("invalid HTTP chunk size".into());}
        let count=usize::from_str_radix(size,16).map_err(|_|"invalid HTTP chunk size")?;
        wire=&wire[line+2..];
        if count==0{if wire!=b"\r\n"{return Err("unexpected HTTP chunk trailer".into());}return Ok(out);}
        let end=count.checked_add(2).ok_or("HTTP chunk size overflow")?;
        if wire.len()<end||&wire[count..end]!=b"\r\n"{return Err("incomplete HTTP chunk body".into());}
        out.extend_from_slice(&wire[..count]);wire=&wire[end..];
    }
}
fn rotation_successor<'a>(session:&serde_json::Value,rotated:&'a serde_json::Value)->Result<&'a str,String>{
    let field=|value:&serde_json::Value,name:&str|->Result<String,String>{
        value["session"][name].as_str().filter(|v|!v.trim().is_empty()).map(str::to_owned).ok_or_else(||format!("session response lacks a nonempty {name}"))
    };
    if field(session,"principal")?!=field(rotated,"principal")?{return Err("rotation changed the reported principal".into());}
    let token=field(session,"token")?;let next=field(rotated,"token")?;
    if token==next{return Err("rotation did not return a distinct successor".into());}
    Ok(rotated["session"]["token"].as_str().expect("checked nonempty successor"))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests{
    #[test]fn rotation_requires_explicit_distinct_same_principal_credentials(){
        use serde_json::json;
        let original=json!({"session":{"principal":"guest:one","token":"first"}});
        let next=json!({"session":{"principal":"guest:one","token":"second"}});
        assert_eq!(super::rotation_successor(&original,&next).unwrap(),"second");
        for principal in [serde_json::Value::Null,json!(""),json!(" "),json!(17),json!({}),json!([]),json!("guest:other")]{
            let malformed=json!({"session":{"principal":principal,"token":"second"}});
            assert!(super::rotation_successor(&original,&malformed).is_err());
            assert!(super::rotation_successor(&malformed,&next).is_err());
        }
        assert!(super::rotation_successor(&json!({"session":{"token":"first"}}),&json!({"session":{"token":"second"}})).is_err());
        assert!(super::rotation_successor(&original,&original).is_err());
    }
    #[test]fn production_schema_chunks_are_decoded_before_json(){
        let body=super::chunks(b"5\r\n{\"a\":\r\n6\r\n\"yes\"}\r\n0\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&body).unwrap()["a"],"yes");
        assert!(super::chunks(b"10\r\nshort\r\n0\r\n\r\n").is_err());
        assert!(super::chunks(b"ffffffffffffffffffff\r\n").is_err());
        assert_eq!(super::chunks(b"1\r\n\xc3\r\n1\r\n\xa9\r\n0\r\n\r\n").unwrap(),"é".as_bytes());
        let prefix=b"{\"denied\":{\"code\":\"E_AUTH\"}}";
        assert!(super::framed("HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\nContent-Length: 100",prefix).is_err(),"complete JSON prefix must not hide truncation");
        assert!(super::framed("HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\nContent-Length: 2",b"{}").is_err());
        assert!(super::framed("HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\nContent-Length: 2",b"{}").is_err());
        assert_eq!(super::framed("HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 2",b"{}").unwrap(),b"{}");
        assert!(super::framed("HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\nContent-Length : 100",prefix).is_err());
        assert!(super::framed("HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\nContent-Length: \u{00a0}2",b"{}").is_err());
        assert!(super::chunks(b"+2\r\n{}\r\n0\r\n\r\n").is_err());
    }
}

// An actual credential history at the production admission boundary. A stale
// device's already copied bytes remain a separate, explicitly unproved path.
fn revoked_sessions(bound:&Bound,address:SocketAddr)->Result<usize,String>{
    if !bound.schema.auth.guests && !bound.schema.auth.password {
        println!("security not covered: ordinary session revocation HTTP requires declared guest or password identity");return Ok(0);
    }
    let (path,payload)=if bound.schema.auth.guests{("/auth/guest","{}")}
        else{("/auth/signup","{\"email\":\"security-campaign@example.test\",\"password\":\"Isolated-campaign-7842!\"}")};
    let (status,session)=exchange(address,"POST",path,"",payload)?;
    if status!=200{return Err(format!("private revocation campaign could not authenticate (HTTP {status}); no revocation claim"));}
    let token=session["session"]["token"].as_str().ok_or("private revocation campaign did not receive a session")?;
    let bearer=format!("Authorization: Bearer {token}\r\n");
    if exchange(address,"GET","/schema",&bearer,"")?.0!=200{return Err("fresh session failed schema admission before revocation".into());}
    let (status,rotated)=exchange(address,"POST","/auth/refresh",&bearer,"{}")?;
    if status!=200{return Err("private revocation campaign could not rotate its live session".into());}
    let successor=rotation_successor(&session,&rotated)?;
    let next=format!("Authorization: Bearer {successor}\r\n");
    if exchange(address,"GET","/schema",&next,"")?.0!=200{return Err("rotation successor failed admission before revocation".into());}
    let (status,retired)=exchange(address,"GET","/schema",&bearer,"")?;
    if status!=401||retired["denied"]["code"]!="E_AUTH"{return Err("rotation left the original credential admitted before logout-all".into());}
    let (status,ended)=exchange(address,"POST","/auth/logout-all",&next,"{}")?;
    if status!=200||ended["ok"]!=true||ended["revoked"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0)==0{return Err("private revocation campaign could not revoke its successor".into());}
    let mut attempted=0;
    for header in [&bearer,&next] {
        let mut paths=vec![("GET","/schema".to_owned()),("GET","/changes?since=0&wait=0".to_owned()),("POST","/sync".to_owned()),("POST","/probe".to_owned()),("POST","/auth/refresh".to_owned())];
        for p in &bound.programs{
            let prefix=match p.program.kind{snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Query=>"q",snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation=>"m",_=>continue};
            paths.push(("POST",format!("/{prefix}/{}",p.program.name)));
        }
        for (method,path) in paths{
            let (status,body)=exchange(address,method,&path,header,"{\"id\":\"security-revoked\",\"args\":{}}")?;attempted+=1;
            if status!=401||body["denied"]["code"]!="E_AUTH"{return Err(format!("revoked credential admitted at {method} {path} (HTTP {status}); bearer omitted"));}
        }
    }
    println!("security live-session revocation HTTP: {attempted} attempts refused after actual refresh and logout-all; disconnected device storage is not covered");
    Ok(attempted)
}
