pub fn check(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    if options.flags.contains_key("audit") { return security::audit(options); }
    if options.flags.contains_key("store") && options.flags.contains_key("release") {
        return releases::check_release(options);
    }

    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get("emit-lean") {
        return emit_lean(file);
    }
    let project = project_or_exit();
    let started = Instant::now();
    package_versions::warn(&project);
    upgrade_history::report(&project, options.json);
    if options.flags.contains_key("fix") {
        if let Err(code) = fix::checked(fix::compile(&project)) { return code; }
    }
    let compiled_sources = match project.sources() {
        Ok(sources) => sources,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("cannot read snapback/: {error}"); return 2; }
    };
    let mut bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&compiled_sources);
    let reviewable_notes = bound.notes.iter().map(note_reviews::fingerprint).collect();
    for warning in saved_fixture_drift::warnings(&project) {
        if warning.drift { eprintln!("check: WARNING: saved fixture drift: {}", warning.message); }
        else { eprintln!("check: info: {}", warning.message); }
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("fix") && bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        if let Err(error) = fix_fixtures::run(&project, &bound, options) { failed_check(&bound, options.json, "E_INPUT", format!("check --fix: {error}")); return 2; }
    }

## 3. Semantics

### 3.1 Time

- Deadlines use the server clock (`Shared::now`). That is the development
  clock under `dev`, so `dev clock advance` expires ephemeral rows as it
  expires `expire at` rows.
- A row is absent at `now >= deadline`, through the same `expired` check the
  runner already applies to expiring tables. The engine gives ephemeral
  tables a hidden `$deadline` column and an `$expiry` index, like
  `through`'s hidden deadline (`schema/through.rs`).
- **`$deadline` never reaches the wire.**
  - `recorded.rs` `public_image` returns a row unchanged when the table has
    no recorded fields, no `through` and no timeline slots. Ordinary
    ephemeral tables have exactly that shape. That fast path must also
    check for engine fields.
  - `value/disclosed.rs` `Disclosed::visible` must hide `$deadline` as it
    hides recorded fields.
  - Wire tests cover unshaped point reads, scans, and rows returned by
    mutations.
- Generated row types omit engine fields (`emit.rs:24`).

### 3.2 Authorization and revocation

- Ephemeral rows are read under their table's read rule, like any row.
- Rules are evaluated at read time, and ephemeral rows are never cached on a
  device. So a principal who loses membership sees nothing on their next
  read of that room.
- A durable commit to a table a watched query probes invalidates that
  query, as today.
- By the renewal law (§2.2), a departed member's rows outlive the departure
  by at most one life, whatever the update rule says.
- **Disclosure.** The `/changes` answer names ephemeral tables that were
  written. That is the same class as today's table-granular durable
  invalidation, which names every table a commit touched (`serve_changes.rs:80-94`).
  It adds no new leak class. Capacity refusals depend only on the writer's
  own rows (§3.5), except for the global backstop, which reveals only a
  server-wide aggregate, like HTTP admission.

### 3.3 Change notification

**NOT READY** for commit `60eeb9a4a8515b096884b34c453caccde5d3d824`, reviewed against `4be5ffa59e`.

1. **P1: A payload column named `role` bypasses authored creators.** [generated.rs:87](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/generated.rs:87) classifies any such table as a roster, seeds invented rows, and skips its creator. This valid source makes both `check` and `test --fixture-only` fail:

   ```quarry
   table reports:
     role: enum('draft','final')
     approved: bool
     read <- allow
     insert <- allow
     online only
   mutation file():
     return insert reports {role: 'draft', approved: true}
   invariant reports always .approved
   ```

   Generated rows contain `approved: false`, producing two `GeneratedFixtureTrace` violations. Renaming `role` to `category` executes the creator, preserves `approved: true`, and removes the violations. Roster discovery must establish an actual principal/authority relationship.

2. **P2: The operation-specific dispatch still executes unsupported ephemeral mutations.** [challenges.rs:301](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges.rs:301) lacks the ephemeral filter. Repro: declare durable `docs`, an ordinary ephemeral `pulses` table keyed by principal, and:

   ```quarry
   mutation ping():
     upsert pulses[viewer] {}
   invariant docs.ping only deny
   ```

   `challenges::run` reports **4 attempted, 0 refused**, without an ephemeral coverage gap, despite having no supported operation. This path bypasses the central paired-observer backstop. Filter it before execution and counting.

3. **P2: The HTTP checker falsely rejects valid ephemeral authentication refusals.** [http.rs:198](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/security/http.rs:198) requires `401 denied.E_AUTH` for every mutation. A safe password-enabled mixed app with the `ping` above and a durable query makes `test --fixture-only` exit 1 because `/m/ping` correctly returns **HTTP 200, `state: failed`, `why.code: E_AUTH`**. Recognize the existing ephemeral refusal envelope; preserve live wire semantics.

The last two paths predate this delta but remain gaps against the requested final contract.

Validation passed: **18 language controls, 18 core ephemeral controls, 7 CLI controls, and 2 affected history controls**. Additional disposable probes reproduced all three findings. No repository source files changed.

This is a source-readiness verdict. Separately running full cross-platform qualification is not credited here, and no publication readiness is claimed.
